### [1] [SceneBind: Binding What and Where Across Vision, Audio and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15265)
*Mingfei Chen,Zijun Cui,Ruoke Zhang,Hyeonggon Ryu,Eli Shlizerman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneBind proposes an omni-modal representation for scenes that jointly captures semantic and 3D spatial information across vision, audio, and language. It uses a semantic-spatial representation combining global semantic embedding with object-centric slots, and introduces a matching scheme for cross-modal retrieval and object grounding. A new binaural audio-visual dataset with structured annotations is curated. SceneBind achieves SOTA on scene and spatial retrieval, and enables zero-shot transfer to audio-visual localization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing omni-modal encoders focus on instance-level semantics (what is present) but lack explicit spatial structure (where it is). SceneBind aims to bridge this gap by representing scenes as semantic-spatial entities.

Method: SceneBind represents each scene as a combination of a global semantic embedding and object-centric semantic-spatial slots. It uses a semantic-spatial matching scheme (SceneBind Matching) that integrates global scene similarity with object alignment for cross-modal retrieval and grounding. Training uses a novel real-world binaural audio-visual dataset with structured semantic and spatial annotations. The model is compatible with large-scale pretrained semantic encoders and adds lightweight spatial modeling with few additional tokens.

Result: SceneBind achieves state-of-the-art performance on scene and spatial retrieval tasks, and demonstrates strong zero-shot transfer to downstream tasks such as audio-visual localization.

Conclusion: SceneBind provides an effective omni-modal representation that integrates semantics and 3D spatial understanding, enabling cross-modal retrieval and grounding. The curated dataset and training protocol facilitate alignment across modalities.

Abstract: We present SceneBind, an omni-modal representation of realistic scenes with joint semantic and 3D spatial understanding across vision, audio and language. Existing omni-modal encoders excel at instance-level semantics (i.e., what is present), but often lack explicit spatial structure (i.e., where it is). SceneBind addresses this gap by representing each scene as a semantic-spatial entity, combining a global semantic embedding with object-centric semantic-spatial slots. This representation explicitly captures object-level semantics, spatial attributes, and uncertainty. We further propose SceneBind Matching, a semantic-spatial matching scheme that integrates global scene similarity with object alignment, supporting cross-modal scene retrieval and object grounding. To train and evaluate SceneBind, we curate a novel real-world binaural audio-visual dataset with structured semantic and spatial annotations, and propose a training protocol for aligning semantic and spatial signals across modalities. SceneBind is compatible with large-scale pretrained semantic encoders, adds lightweight spatial modeling with only a few additional tokens. It achieves state-of-the-art scene and spatial retrieval while enabling strong zero-shot transfer to downstream tasks such as audio-visual localization.

</details>


### [2] [MultiRef-Compass: Towards Comprehensive Evaluation of Multi-Reference-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14189)
*Xiaohan Zhang,Yuqing Wen,Junlin Chen,Yuqi Tang,Yiting He,Lizhuo Shao,Weiming Zhu,Tengfei Liu,Yang Shi,Jialu Chen,Yuanxing Zhang,Huaxiong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MultiRef-Compass，一个面向多参考到音频-视频（MR2AV）生成任务的统一基准。该基准包含 350 个精心构建的样本，涵盖多视角主体保持、多实体绑定和人-物-场景组合；定义了包含 14 个子指标的四维度评估协议（基本质量、参考一致性、音视频一致性、指令遵循），并整合了自动指标与重新评判增强的 MLLM-as-a-Judge 框架。对 8 个代表性 MR2AV 系统的实验表明，现有方法在多个维度上仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注文本驱动生成、单参考主体保持或孤立的音视频对齐，尚未覆盖多参考到音视频生成（MR2AV）这一新兴场景。MR2AV 要求模型同时推理多个参考并生成同步的视觉和音频内容，缺乏统一的评估基准。

Method: 通过可扩展且可控的资产组合流水线构建 350 个样本，覆盖多视角主体保持、多实体绑定和人-物-场景组合；定义包含 14 个子指标的四维度评估协议（基本质量、参考一致性、音视频一致性、指令遵循）；整合自动指标与重新评判增强的 MLLM-as-a-Judge 框架，实现可扩展且可审计的评估。

Result: 在 8 个代表性 MR2AV 系统上的大量实验显示，现有方法在多个评估维度上仍有显著改进空间。

Conclusion: MultiRef-Compass 可作为未来 MR2AV 研究的基础基准，弥补了该领域缺乏统一评估的空白。

Abstract: Multi-reference-to-audio-video (MR2AV) generation aims to generate coherent audio-video content conditioned on multiple references and textual instructions. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on text-driven generation, single-reference subject preservation, or isolated audio-video alignment, leaving the emerging MR2AV setting largely unexplored. Compared with these settings, MR2AV requires models to jointly reason over multiple references while generating synchronized visual and audio content. Models must not only preserve each reference faithfully but also correctly bind and compose multiple referenced entities into coherent audio-visual events. To address this gap, we introduce MultiRef-Compass, a unified benchmark for MR2AV generation. It comprises $350$ carefully curated samples constructed through a scalable and controllable asset-composition pipeline, covering multi-view subject preservation, multi-entity binding, and human-object-scene composition. To provide interpretable assessment, MultiRef-Compass defines an evaluation protocol with four dimensions: Basic Quality, Reference Consistency, Audio-Visual Consistency, and Instruction Following, using 14 sub-metrics. MultiRef-Compass integrates automatic metrics with a rejudging-enhanced MLLM-as-a-Judge framework, enabling scalable and auditable evaluation of both perceptual fidelity and reference-conditioned composition. Extensive experiments on eight representative MR2AV systems reveal substantial room for improvement across multiple evaluation dimensions, underscoring the need for a comprehensive benchmark and positioning MultiRef-Compass as a foundation for future MR2AV research.

</details>


### [3] [Inference-Time Concept Suppression and Video-Centric Evaluation for Text-to-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14194)
*Wenxuan Chen,Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIRUS 是一个无需训练、推理时运行的概念级文本到视频遗忘框架。它通过定位并抑制目标概念在采样过程中的表达，在不更新编码器或去噪网络的前提下实现概念擦除。在 CogVideoX 和 Wan2.2 上的实验表明，SIRUS 在遗忘成功率、帧命中率、视频质量保持等方面优于现有方法，并在遗忘-质量权衡上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频生成器能合成逼真视频，但难以可控地移除特定概念。与文本到图像的概念擦除不同，视频遗忘需要抑制跨帧持续的目标概念，同时保留非目标主体、动作、场景和时间结构。现有方法在遗忘效果与视频质量之间难以取得良好平衡。

Method: SIRUS 是一个训练无关的推理时框架，给定目标概念的文本别名，它在采样过程中定位与目标相关的提示证据并抑制其表达，无需更新文本编码器或去噪网络。同时提出了一个专门针对视频遗忘的评估框架，分别测量目标遗忘、非目标保留、视频质量、越狱鲁棒性和效率。

Result: 在 CogVideoX 上对五个安全、物体和风格概念进行测试，SIRUS 达到 70.4% 的平均遗忘成功率和 25.7% 的平均帧命中率，而 VideoEraser 为 44.4%/47.2%；同时将平均 VBench 质量下降从 -0.043 降低到 -0.016，实现了最佳的遗忘-质量权衡。在 Wan2.2 上的迁移实验表明 SIRUS 能泛化到现代 T2V 骨干网络。

Conclusion: SIRUS 是一种有效的、无需训练的概念级视频遗忘方法，能在不牺牲视频质量的前提下实现较高的遗忘成功率，并且具有良好的跨模型泛化能力。其提出的视频遗忘评估框架也为该领域提供了标准化评测手段。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generators can synthesize realistic and temporally coherent videos, but controllably removing a target concept from a generator remains difficult.
  Unlike text-to-image concept erasure, T2V unlearning must suppress a target concept that may persist across frames while preserving non-target subjects, actions, scenes, and temporal structure.
  We propose \textbf{SIRUS}, a training-free inference-time framework for concept-level T2V unlearning.
  Given textual aliases of a target concept, SIRUS localizes target-related prompt evidence and suppresses target expression during sampling, without updating the text encoder or denoising network.
  We further introduce a video-oriented evaluation framework for T2V unlearning that separately measures target forgetting, non-target preservation, video quality, jailbreak robustness, and efficiency, using video-level failure criteria, frame-level residue statistics, paired preservation analysis, VBench-based quality diagnostics, and deployment overhead measurement.
  Across five safety, object, and style concepts on CogVideoX, SIRUS reaches 70.4\% average forgetting success and 25.7\% average frame hit, compared with 44.4\% / 47.2\% for VideoEraser, while reducing the average VBench quality drop from -0.043 to -0.016, yielding the strongest forgetting-quality trade-off among fully evaluated baselines.
  Transfer experiments on Wan2.2 further suggest that SIRUS generalizes across modern T2V backbones.

</details>


### [4] [KeyFrame-Compass: Towards Comprehensive Evaluation of Keyframe-Conditioned Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14202)
*Yuqi Tang,Tengfei Liu,Yizheng Lai,Yuran Wang,Yang Shi,Wanshun Su,Zhuoran Zhang,Qixun Wang,Xiaohan Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Xuehai Bai,Xuanyu Zhu,Bohan Zeng,Bozhou Li,Shujie Li,Yifan Dai,Yujie Wei,Shixuan Liu,Haotian Wang,Jialu Chen,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KeyFrame-Compass 是首个针对关键帧条件视频生成的综合基准，包含 386 个精心设计的样本和自动化评估框架，对九个代表性视频生成系统进行实验，揭示了当前模型在关键帧执行与视频自然性之间的权衡、随关键帧密度增加性能下降，以及开源模型无法正确理解故事板网格输入等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有模型支持多关键帧条件，但它们能否在保持整体视频质量的同时忠实地再现给定的关键帧仍不清楚，因此需要系统性的评估基准来衡量关键帧条件视频生成的表现。

Method: 构建了 KeyFrame-Compass 基准，包含 386 个样本，涵盖三个应用领域、两种视频结构、两种提示粒度、两种条件格式和四种关键帧密度；提出了自动化评估框架，通过六项互补指标（存在性、保真度、时间顺序、定位、持久性和唯一性）衡量关键帧执行情况，并利用基于证据的多模态大语言模型判断和专用感知模型评估整体视频质量。

Result: 在九个代表性视频生成系统上的实验表明：当前模型在忠实执行关键帧与自然视频合成之间存在明显权衡；随着关键帧约束变密，性能下降；大多数开源模型无法将故事板网格输入解释为按时间排序的关键帧序列。

Conclusion: 现有视频生成模型在处理关键帧条件时存在根本性局限，无法同时保证关键帧的准确执行和视频的自然流畅性。

Abstract: Video generation increasingly relies on keyframe-based workflows, where creators specify a sequence of reference images to guide generation. Although recent models support multi-keyframe conditioning, it remains unclear whether they can faithfully reproduce the prescribed keyframes while maintaining overall video quality. We present KeyFrame-Compass, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating keyframe-conditioned video generation. The benchmark contains 386 carefully curated samples spanning three application domains, two video structures, two prompt granularities, two conditioning formats, and four keyframe densities, enabling controlled analysis under diverse generation settings. We further introduce an automated evaluation framework that jointly measures keyframe execution and overall video quality. Specifically, we decompose keyframe execution into six complementary metrics covering presence, fidelity, temporal ordering, localization, persistence, and uniqueness, while assessing overall video quality through evidence-grounded MLLM judgments augmented with specialized perception models. Experiments on nine representative video generation systems reveal several fundamental limitations. Current models exhibit a clear trade-off between faithful keyframe execution and natural video synthesis. Their performance further degrades as keyframe constraints become denser and most open-source models also fail to interpret storyboard-grid inputs as temporally ordered keyframe sequences.

</details>


### [5] [SeeSE3: Emergence of 3D Space in Vision Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14228)
*Caroline Chen,Sayna Ebrahimi,Fedor Kitashov,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Leonidas Guibas,Viorica Pătrăucean,Maks Ovsjanikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉基础模型是否构建了反映三维欧几里得空间内在属性的表示。不同于以往通过回归深度或法线等图像中心量来探测3D感知的方法，本文从拓扑和几何两个角度提出了新的探测方法：互邻域度量和庞加莱适配器，用于评估特征空间结构与SE(3)变换群的关系。结果表明，自监督视觉模型在正确探测下存在与三维欧几里得空间高度相关的潜在子空间。基于此，本文提出了“潜在空间导航”技术，可直接在潜在空间中执行视觉里程计和定位，无需显式3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多通过回归深度/法线等图像中心量来探测视觉特征的3D感知能力，但少有人直接研究视觉特征空间的结构与三维欧几里得空间变换群（SE(3)）之间的内在关系。作者希望探明自监督视觉模型（未经过直接3D监督或主动代理训练）是否在潜在空间中编码了3D空间的拓扑与几何结构，并利用这一性质实现无需显式3D重建的导航。

Method: 提出了两种探测方法：(1) 互邻域度量（Mutual Neighborhood Metric），用于衡量特征邻域与空间拓扑的对齐程度；(2) 庞加莱适配器（Poincaré Adapter），用于测试静态场景中潜在位移对相机运动几何的线性可访问性。这些方法从拓扑和几何两个视角评估视觉特征空间与SE(3)的关系。

Result: 在正确探测条件下，自监督视觉模型（如对比学习、掩蔽图像建模等）的潜在子空间与三维欧几里得空间存在强相关性，表明这些模型内隐地学习了3D空间的结构。

Conclusion: 基于上述发现，作者提出了“潜在空间导航”（Latent-Space Navigation）技术，可在纯潜在空间中完成视觉里程计和定位，绕过显式的3D重建步骤，为无需深度传感器或3D监督的视觉导航提供了新途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we ask whether vision foundation models construct representations that reflect the intrinsic properties of 3D Euclidean space. Unlike previous works that probe 3D awareness of vision features by regressing image-centric quantities such as depth or normals, we investigate the relation between the structure of the space of visual features and the group of Euclidean transformations $SE(3)$. We propose a set of probes to evaluate this relation from both topological and geometric perspectives: a mutual neighborhood metric that measures the alignment between feature neighborhoods and spatial topology, and a Poincaré Adapter to test the linear accessibility of the geometry of camera motion from latent displacements in static scenes. We show that self-supervised vision models, which, in principle, have not been trained with direct 3D supervision or active agency, possess latent subspaces that are remarkably strongly correlated with three-dimensional Euclidean space, when probed correctly. Building on this insight we propose a new class of "Latent-Space Navigation" techniques that perform visual odometry and localization purely in the latent space, bypassing the need for explicit 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [6] [3D Lane Detection with Odometry for High-Speed Vehicle Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14248)
*Omoruyi Atekha,John Subosits,Marcus Greiff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于赛车场景的3D车道检测数据集和方法，通过多摄像头集成和惯性测量预积分，实现了近300Hz的处理速度，F1分数超过0.9，横向平均绝对误差低于0.18米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规驾驶场景下的车道检测已被广泛研究，但在赛车场景中，车辆速度更高、道路几何形状更极端，现有方法难以适用。因此需要专门针对赛车环境的高效3D车道检测方案。

Method: 1) 构建了一个包含超过25万张图像、多摄像头馈送和惯性测量数据的新数据集（在封闭赛道上用Lexus LC 500采集）。2) 对比多种3D车道检测方法，并提出改进使其处理帧率接近300Hz。3) 采用多摄像头集成方法，并利用惯性测量进行预积分，跨摄像头和时间回归道路几何形状。

Result: 与BevLaneDet相比，加入里程计和集成预测后F1分数提升3个百分点，近车平均绝对误差降低超过30%；实际部署中F1>0.9，横向MAE<0.18m。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在赛车高速场景下实现高效、高精度的3D车道检测，并在硬件上验证了可行性。

Abstract: Lane boundary detection is a critical component in autonomous driving systems and has been rigorously studied in regular driving scenarios. However, it is less explored in vehicle racing, where the car moves at higher speeds across more extreme road geometries. To study this problem, we introduce a new dataset for 3D lane detection in racing, featuring >$250$k images from multiple camera feeds and inertial measurements taken with a Lexus LC 500 driving on a closed circuit. With this dataset, we compare various approaches to 3D lane detection and propose modifications that permit frames to be processed at rates of almost 300Hz while retaining high predictive performance in the racing application. This facilitates a multi-camera ensemble approach that is validated on hardware. We show that sensing modalities such as inertial measurements can be leveraged for pre-integration to regress road geometries over both cameras and time, yielding improvements in key metrics. Compared to methods such as BevLaneDet, adding odometry and ensemble predictions improves the F1 score by 3 points and reduces near-vehicle mean absolute errors (MAEs) by $>30 \%$. We show F1 scores $>$0.9 and lateral MAEs of $<$0.18m in vehicle deployments.

</details>


### [7] [MonteRET: AI Agent Enhancing Multimodal LLMs with Multi-granularity Knowledge Retrieval for Chest CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14264)
*Yi Lin,Yihao Ding,Elana Benishay,Elefterios Trikantzopoulos,David Nauheim,Hanley Ong,Jiang Bian,Hua Xu,Yuzhe Yang,George Shih,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MonteRET，一种区域感知的检索增强框架，用于自动生成胸部CT报告。该方法整合全局和局部CT特征，通过预测的医疗条件和区域级视觉-语言对齐检索临床相关知识，并利用知识引导的报告重写代理优化初始报告。在RadGenome-ChestCT的24,128个CT扫描上训练，在1,564个测试集和82个外部CT扫描上评估，相比基线方法和多种SOTA方法在报告质量、语义相似度和临床效能上均有提升，召回率提升最显著，放射科住院医师的人工评估也更偏好MonteRET。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床可靠的胸部CT报告生成需要同时理解整体体积和准确描述局部解剖发现，现有方法难以做到区域感知的精准描述，因此需要一种能够整合全局与局部信息并利用检索增强知识的方法。

Method: MonteRET框架包含三个核心组件：1) 整合全局CT特征与区域级解剖表示；2) 利用预测的医疗条件和区域级视觉-语言对齐检索临床相关知识；3) 通过知识引导的报告重写代理对初始报告进行精炼。

Result: 在RadGenome-ChestCT的1,564个测试集和纽约长老会/威尔康奈尔医学中心的82个外部CT扫描上评估，MonteRET在报告质量、语义相似度和临床效能方面均优于匹配基线和多种SOTA方法，召回率提升最为显著（表明遗漏发现更少），放射科住院医师的人工评估也更偏好MonteRET。

Conclusion: MonteRET通过区域感知和检索增强策略有效提升了胸部CT报告生成的临床准确性和完整性，尤其减少了遗漏发现的情况，是一种有前景的自动化报告生成框架。

Abstract: Automated chest CT report generation remains challenging because clinically faithful reporting requires both whole-volume understanding and accurate description of localized anatomical findings. Here we developed and retrospectively evaluated MonteRET, a region-aware retrieval-enhanced framework for generating chest CT findings sections. MonteRET integrates global CT features with region-level anatomical representations, retrieves clinically relevant knowledge using predicted medical conditions and region-level vision-language alignment, and refines initial reports through a knowledge-guided report rewriting agent. We trained our model on a public cohort with 24,128 CT scans from RadGenome-ChestCT. We evaluated MonteRET on the public RadGenome-ChestCT test set of 1,564 CT scans and an external cohort of 82 CT scans from NewYork-Presbyterian/Weill Cornell Medical Center. MonteRET improved report quality, semantic similarity, and clinical efficacy compared with a matched baseline and several state-of-the-art methods. Gains were most pronounced for recall, suggesting fewer omitted findings. Human expert evaluation by radiology residents also favored MonteRET.

</details>


### [8] [XCT-SAM: Sequential Parameter-Efficient Domain Adaptation of SAM for Industrial XCT Defect Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14287)
*Md Mahedi Hasan,Md Mushfiqur Rahaman,Alan Pachkovskiy,Imtiaz Ahmed,Jeremy Dawson,Srinjoy Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XCT-SAM，一种通过顺序参数高效适配框架（先在中尺度合金微观结构数据上微调Conv-LoRA，再迁移至XCT图像），解决增材制造XCT图像中缺陷分割面临的类别不平衡和域偏移问题，在零样本SAM和域适配SAM基线中取得了最优的IoU和Dice分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造XCT图像中缺陷分割面临严重的类别不平衡和扫描条件间的分布偏移；SAM等基础模型在自然图像上的预训练难以迁移到AM XCT领域，因为缺陷表现为微弱的非语义微观结构异常；域差距大且标记的真实XCT数据稀缺。

Method: 提出XCT-SAM顺序参数高效适配框架：首先在合金微观结构数据集上微调Conv-LoRA适配器（注入卷积空间归纳偏置），然后将适配后的模型迁移至XCT图像，逐步缩小域差距；使用秩r=2的Conv-LoRA，训练约4.15M参数，冻结超过99%的模型参数。

Result: 在分布外CycleGAN-XCT基准和真实NIST XCT扫描上，XCT-SAM一致优于零样本SAM和其他域适配SAM基线，取得了最佳的整体IoU和Dice分数。

Conclusion: 中间域适配结合参数高效适配器对于工业XCT缺陷分割是有效的，为将基础模型迁移至数据稀缺的工业CT领域提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Defect segmentation in additive manufacturing (AM) X-ray computed tomography (XCT) images remains challenging due to severe class imbalance and large distribution shifts across scan conditions. Although recent foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) provide strong general-purpose segmentation priors, their natural-image pre-training transfers poorly to the AM XCT domain, where defects appear as subtle non-semantic microstructural anomalies. Moreover, adapting SAM to the AM domain is further limited by the large domain gap and scarcity of labeled real XCT data. We present XCT-SAM, a sequential parameter-efficient adaptation framework for AM XCT defect segmentation. Instead of adapting SAM directly from natural images to XCT data, we first fine-tune Conv-LoRA adapters on an alloy-microstructure dataset and subsequently transfer the adapted model to XCT images, progressively bridging the domain gap. Using Conv-LoRA adapters with rank r=2, the framework injects convolutional spatial inductive bias into SAM's backbone while training approximately 4.15M parameters and keeping over 99% of the model frozen. We evaluate XCT-SAM on out-of-distribution CycleGAN-XCT benchmarks and real-world NIST XCT scans. Across both settings, XCT-SAM consistently outperforms zero-shot SAM and other domain-adapted SAM baselines, achieving the best overall IoU and Dice scores. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of intermediate domain adaptation with parameter-efficient adapters for industrial XCT defect segmentation. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Mahedi-61/XCT-SAM.git

</details>


### [9] [DCVC-MB: Neural B-Frame Video Compression using State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14305)
*Arjun Arora,Calvin-Khang Ta,Carlos Restrepo-Galeano,Kruthi Murali,Naga Akhil E S,Arunkumar Mohananchettiar,Jay Shingala,Tong Shao,Peng Yin,Sean McCarthy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DCVC-Mamba（DCVC-MB），一种用于 B 帧编码的神经视频编解码器框架，融合了 IBP 帧策略、基于状态空间模型的时空融合模型、熵感知跳过机制以及两种推理时增强策略，在多个基准上实现了显著的 BD 率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经视频编码（NVC）在 B 帧编码场景中仍存在效率与压缩性能不足的问题，亟需更有效的双向时间预测与熵编码加速机制。

Method: 1) 采用 IBP 帧策略实现低延迟 B 帧编码；2) 基于状态空间模型（SSM）构建时空融合模型，用于双向时间预测；3) 设计熵感知跳过机制，选择性跳过某些潜在特征的编码以降低熵编码时间；4) 引入两种推理时策略进一步提升压缩性能。

Result: 与先前的神经视频编码器相比，平均 BD 率降低达 8.98%；相较于 VTM-19.0-LDP 和 VTM-19.0-RA（Inter-GoP=16）基准，分别提升 30.45% 和 1.81%。

Conclusion: DCVC-MB 在 B 帧编码任务上优于现有 NVC 和传统编解码器，推动了神经视频压缩领域的进展。

Abstract: In this paper we propose DCVC-Mamba (DCVC-MB), a neural video codec framework for B-frame coding. Our approach incorporates an IBP frame strategy for low-delay B-frame coding, a spatio-temporal fusion model based on state-space models for bidirectional temporal prediction, and an entropy-aware skipping mechanism that selectively omits coding certain latents to reduce entropy coding times. In addition to our model contributions we also implement two inference-time strategies that enhance compression performance. Experimental evaluation shows that DCVC-MB compares favorably to existing NVCs and traditional codecs. The method demonstrates BD-rate reductions of up to $8.98\%$ on average compared to prior neural video codecs, and improvements of up to $30.45\%$ and $1.81\%$ over the VTM-19.0-LDP and VTM-19.0-RA(Inter-GoP=16) benchmarks, respectively, contributing to advances in neural video compression.

</details>


### [10] [SD-MAR: Multi-image Analytical Reasoning via Synthetic Data and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14333)
*Shiyu Yuan,Sourav Sanjukta Bhabesh,Zhe Wang,Dmitriy Bespalov,Wesley Rose,Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SD-MAR框架，通过构建配对视觉场景的受控扰动生成多图像分析推理任务，并采用去KL正则化的GRPO-lite强化学习方法（配合BDA后向折扣分配）微调VLM，显著提升多图像比较、变化检测等分析推理能力，在多个基准上保持或提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在需要跨多视觉状态进行分析推理的任务（如多图像比较、变化检测、多步视觉推理）中能力有限，而现有基准很少同时要求显式视觉比较和分析推理，这一能力缺口尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 构建SD-MAR数据集：通过受控扰动生成配对视觉场景，覆盖语义变化归因和定量比较等推理任务。2) 提出GRPO-lite（去除KL正则化）配合BDA（对后续推理步骤赋予更大信用）的强化学习微调方法，强化策略优化。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B和InternVL3-8B上微调后，域内准确率提升最高36.95%；Qwen2.5-VL-7B在SD-MAR基准上超越GPT-4.1；域外泛化保持或提升（MME/MMMU-Pro/MathVista波动<1%，MMBench提升4%）；LLM评判显示逻辑一致性和解释质量持续提升。

Conclusion: SD-MAR框架结合GRPO-lite微调能有效弥补VLM在多图像分析推理方面的不足，且不牺牲已有能力，为提升VLM的跨视觉状态推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong perceptual abilities but remain limited in tasks requiring analytical reasoning across multiple visual states, such as multi-image comparison, change detection, and multi-step visual inference. These capabilities are critical for real-world multimodal applications where reasoning must be grounded in systematic differences between visual contexts. However, existing benchmarks rarely require both explicit visual comparison and analytical reasoning, leaving this capability underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce SD-MAR (Synthetic Data for Multi-image Analytical Reasoning), a framework for training and evaluating VLMs on multi-image analytical reasoning. SD-MAR constructs paired visual scenarios through controlled perturbations and generates reasoning tasks spanning semantic change attribution and quantitative comparison. We further train VLMs using GRPO-lite with Backward Discounted Allocation (BDA), a reinforcement learning approach that removes KL regularization to encourage stronger policy optimization while allocating greater credit to the later reasoning steps where analytical conclusions are formed. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3-8B show that GRPO-lite fine-tuning on SD-MAR improves in-domain accuracy by up to 36.95%, with Qwen2.5-VL-7B outperforming GPT-4.1 on the SD-MAR benchmark. Importantly, out-of-domain generalization is preserved or improved: performance remains within 1% on MME, MMMU-Pro, and MathVista, while improving by up to 4% on MMBench. LLM-as-judge evaluation further demonstrates consistent improvements in logical coherence and explanation quality across both models.

</details>


### [11] [MixCompress: Mixture of Experts for Variable Rate Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14334)
*Calvin-Khang Ta,Praneet Singh,Tong Shao,Peng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixCompress 是一个统一的可变比特率（VBR）图像压缩框架，通过稀疏结构专业化（稀疏门控 MoE + Mixture-of-Depths + 条件辅助变换）解决多率点共享骨干网中的特征纠缠和梯度冲突问题，在计算效率上达到甚至超越单率点基线，建立了新的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型图像压缩（LIC）需要为每个率-失真工作点独立存储模型，开销大。现有VBR方法用密集参数调制迫使共享骨干网逼近不同的映射，导致特征纠缠——低码率平滑梯度与高码率纹理细节保留冲突，性能次优。

Method: 提出 MixCompress，基于稀疏结构专业化：1) 稀疏门控 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 路由缓解梯度冲突；2) Mixture-of-Depths (MoD) 扩展动态缩放模型容量以适应高码率需求；3) 条件辅助变换 (CAT) 进行动态子带能量调制。三者构成层级框架，动态缩放容量。

Result: 大量评估表明 MixCompress 不仅能匹配单独优化的单率点基线，甚至能超越它们，建立了计算高效图像编码的新帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: MixCompress 通过稀疏结构专业化（MoE+MoD+CAT）在统一 VBR 框架中有效解决了多率点冲突，实现了超越单率点基线的性能，为计算高效的图像编码确立了新基准。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) is bottlenecked by the need to store independent models for each rate-distortion operating point. Existing variable bit-rate (VBR) methods aim to reduce this overhead via dense parameter modulation, but forcing a shared backbone to approximate divergent mappings causes severe feature entanglement. Specifically, low-rate smoothing gradients inherently conflict with the preservation of high-frequency textural details, leading to sub-optimal performance. To resolve this, we propose MixCompress, a unified VBR framework based on sparse structural specialization. While sparsely gated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing successfully mitigates gradient conflict, it operates on a fixed computational budget. To address the increased representational demands of higher bit-rates we introduce a Mixture-of-Depths (MoD) extension to dynamically scale model capacity. Combined with Conditional Auxiliary Transforms (CAT) for dynamic sub-band energy modulation, our hierarchical framework effectively dynamically scales capacity. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that MixCompress not only matches individually optimized single-rate baselines but can even surpass them, establishing a new Pareto frontier for computationally efficient image coding.

</details>


### [12] [Beyond scalar losses: calibrating segmentation models via gradient vector field surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14338)
*Laurin Lux,Alexander H. Berger,Moritz Knolle,Daniel Rückert,Johannes C. Paetzold*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出基于区域的损失函数（如Dice损失）在高度类别和区域不平衡的分割任务中虽然表现优异，但会导致模型校准不良和过度自信预测。作者提出一种对梯度向量场的“手术”干预，通过在线性比例上按预测误差缩放梯度幅度来缓解校准问题，在2D和3D医学分割任务中验证了该方法在保持高精度的同时有效改善了校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于区域的损失函数（如Dice损失）在医学图像分割中广泛使用，但模型往往校准不良，产生过度自信的预测，这阻碍了临床采纳，尤其在定义肿瘤切除边界等关键应用中。

Method: 提出一种对损失函数梯度向量场的“手术”干预：在损失函数的偏导数中添加一个因子，使梯度幅度与预测误差呈线性比例缩放，从而修正过度自信问题。

Result: 在2D和3D医学分割任务上的实验表明，该干预方法能有效改善模型校准，同时保持高预测精度，且可与任何基于区域的损失函数结合使用。

Conclusion: 该梯度“手术”是一种简单而有效的干预措施，能够缓解基于区域损失函数的校准问题，有望推动医学分割模型在临床中的采纳。

Abstract: Region-based loss functions, such as the Dice loss, have established themselves as the de facto standard for highly class- and region-imbalanced segmentation tasks. However, models trained using region-based loss functions are notoriously miscalibrated and typically yield over-confident predictions. In medical imaging applications, such as defining tumor resection margins, this miscalibration is hindering clinical adoption. In this work, we outline a novel gradient perspective on this overconfidence and show how it affects region-based loss functions. We propose a "surgery" on the gradient vector field as a simple, yet effective intervention to mitigate calibration issues. This surgery adds a factor to the loss's partial derivative, scaling the gradient's magnitude linearly with the prediction error. In empirical evaluations across 2D and 3D medical segmentation tasks, we demonstrate the effectiveness of this intervention while maintaining high prediction accuracy when used in conjunction with any region-based loss function.

</details>


### [13] [Dynamic Manipulation Hypergraphs for HAR: Beyond Pairwise Relations: Dynamic Manipulation Hypergraphs for Vision-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14350)
*Fatemeh Ziaeetabar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态操作超图框架（Dynamic Manipulation Hypergraph），将细粒度操作识别中的多实体关系建模为高阶关系单元，通过超边候选排序、超图推理网络（节点-超边双向消息传递+时间注意力）和类别无关的超边重要性评分，在EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR和Assembly101上显著优于成对图与静态超图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于图的方法使用成对边（pairwise edges）将协调的操作事件割裂为断开的二元关系，难以捕捉手、物体、工具、支撑面等多实体间的高阶协同关系，因此需要一种能随时间动态建模多实体高阶关系的方法。

Method: 提出动态操作超图框架：1) 每个时间步提取实体的外观、空间、运动、语义角色特征；2) 基于邻近性、接触和运动耦合谓词实例化超边候选并排序；3) 超图推理网络执行节点→超边和超边→节点双向消息传递 + 时间注意力机制；4) 输出类别无关的超边重要性分数，标识模型关注的实体配置和时间区间。

Result: 在EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR上HO-F1比成对图提升6.9个百分点，比静态超图提升4.4个百分点；在Assembly101上分别提升9.5和5.8个百分点；在ARCTIC上定性显示高排名超边与接触密集型操作区间对应。

Conclusion: 时变高阶关系建模对于细粒度操作活动识别具有重要价值，超图框架能够有效捕获多实体间的协同交互，优于传统成对图和静态超图方法。

Abstract: Fine-grained manipulation recognition requires modeling evolving relations among hands, objects, tools, and supporting surfaces. Conventional graph-based methods use pairwise edges that can fragment a coordinated event into disconnected binary relations. We propose a dynamic manipulation hypergraph framework that represents multi-entity configurations as higher-order relational units. At each temporal step, relevant entities are encoded using appearance, spatial, motion, and semantic-role features. Hyperedge candidates are instantiated and ranked using proximity, contact, and motion-coupling predicates. A hypergraph reasoning network performs node-to-hyperedge and hyperedge-to-node message passing, followed by temporal attention over the evolving interaction structure. The framework provides class-agnostic hyperedge-importance scores that identify entity configurations and temporal intervals emphasized by the model without treating them as causal explanations. Quantitative evaluation is conducted on EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR and Assembly101 under an annotation-assisted entity-localization protocol. Video-only and entity-based methods provide contextual comparisons, while a matched pairwise graph and a static hypergraph serve as the principal controlled baselines because they use identical entity inputs and comparable relational settings. The proposed method improves HO-F1 over the matched pairwise graph by 6.9 percentage points on EPIC-KITCHENS-100/VISOR and 9.5 points on Assembly101, and exceeds the static hypergraph by 4.4 and 5.8 points, respectively. Qualitative analysis on ARCTIC further shows correspondence between highly ranked hyperedges and contact-rich manipulation intervals. These results demonstrate the value of time-varying higher-order relational modeling for fine-grained manipulation activity recognition.

</details>


### [14] [$K$-NeAS: Scalable Multi-Material CT Reconstruction Using Neural SDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14415)
*Daksh K. Shah,Emmanouil Nikolakakis,Razvan V. Marinescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: K-NeAS is a unified and scalable architecture for automated multi-material surface reconstruction from sparse-view CT projections, extending implicit scene representations with a shared latent backbone, a differentiable K-material soft selector, automated attenuation bounds via GMM, and a scheduled auxiliary floater loss to reduce hallucinations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing geometry-aware implicit scene representations for sparse-view CT reconstruction are limited by manually tuned attenuation bounds and rigid two-material constraints, preventing automated handling of multiple overlapping tissues.

Method: Proposes K-NeAS: replaces independent material networks with a shared latent backbone and a fully differentiable K-material sequential soft selector; automates attenuation bounding using a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM); implements a scheduled auxiliary floater loss to mitigate geometric hallucinations.

Result: Evaluated on four clinical CBCT datasets, K-NeAS scales to arbitrary material counts, achieving +1.88 dB 3D PSNR improvement over single-material NeAS at K=3 on Abdomen data, and up to +1.17 dB under severe sparsity (5/10-view).

Conclusion: K-NeAS enables automated, multi-material surface reconstruction with superior fidelity and robustness, eliminating manual tuning constraints of prior ISR methods.

Abstract: Computed Tomography (CT) carries significant ionizing radiation risks, driving the need for sparse-view reconstruction. Implicit scene representations (ISRs) address this by recovering continuous volumetric attenuation fields directly from sparse projections, and recent geometry-aware extensions jointly model surface geometry alongside attenuation to improve fidelity and enable clean tissue segmentation without manual thresholding. However, these methods remain limited by manually tuned attenuation bounds and rigid two-material constraints. This paper proposes $K$-NeAS, a unified and scalable architecture for automated, multi-material surface reconstruction. We replace independent material networks with a shared latent backbone and introduce a fully differentiable $K$-material sequential soft selector to model an arbitrary number of overlapping tissues. To eliminate manual tuning, we automate attenuation bounding using a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) and implement a scheduled auxiliary floater loss to mitigate geometric hallucinations common under extreme sparsity. Evaluated across four clinical Cone-Beam CT (CBCT) datasets, $K$-NeAS successfully scales to arbitrary material counts, achieving superior 3D volumetric fidelity at $K=3$ materials on complex multi-tissue regions such as the Abdomen ($33.28\text{ dB}$ 3D PSNR vs. $31.40\text{ dB}$ single-material NeAS baseline, a $+1.88\text{ dB}$ improvement). Furthermore, our model exhibits enhanced robustness under sparse-sampling conditions, outperforming baseline 3D PSNR by up to $1.17\text{ dB}$ under 5- and 10-view constraints.

</details>


### [15] [Emergent Region-Level Facial Correspondence in Frozen Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14423)
*Izaldein Al-Zyoud,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文验证了冻结的DINOv3自监督视觉特征无需人脸专用训练即可作为区域级人脸坐标系统：在跨身份匹配中达到83.0%语义准确率（随机基线23.0%），时间跟踪精度95.5%，且中间层（block 18）比最终层提供更强的对应关系（4.93x vs 1.48x判别比），相较于CLIP在解剖区域上提升16.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索冻结的自监督视觉模型（DINOv3）是否能在不进行人脸专用训练的情况下，建立人类面部的区域级坐标系统，使得不同身份和不同时间下的人脸部件（眼睛、眉毛、鼻子、嘴、皮肤、头发）在特征空间中保持可区分性。

Method: 使用冻结的DINOv3 ViT-L/16 patch嵌入，仅以FaRL作为人脸部件标签接口；在200个CelebDF-v2真实视频上评估跨身份最近邻匹配和时间标签传播性能，并通过消融实验（去除FaRL）和层间对比分析对应关系来源。

Result: DINOv3在跨身份匹配中达到83.0%区域级语义准确率（随机基线23.0%），时间跟踪精度95.5%；无FaRL时准确率降至0.9%；中间层（block 18）的同一区域vs跨区域判别比为4.93x（最终层仅1.48x）；相较于CLIP ViT-L/14在解剖区域上增益16.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 冻结的DINOv3是一种强大的零样本区域级人脸对应表示，且中间层的自监督特征比最终层更适合密集人脸分析任务。

Abstract: Frozen self-supervised vision models can align parts of generic objects, but it remains unclear whether this correspondence extends to human faces, where global layout is shared while identity-specific appearance varies sharply. We test whether frozen DINOv3 features define a region-level facial coordinate system: a feature space in which eyes, brows, nose, mouth, skin, and hair remain distinguishable across people and across time without face-specific training. Using DINOv3 ViT-L/16 patch embeddings and FaRL only as a face-part labeling interface, we evaluate cross-identity nearest-neighbor matching and temporal label propagation on 200 CelebDF-v2 real videos. DINOv3 achieves 83.0% region-level semantic accuracy under unconstrained cross-identity matching, compared with a 23.0% area-weighted random baseline, and 95.5% temporal tracking accuracy without a learned temporal module. A no-FaRL control collapses to 0.9%, showing that FaRL supplies semantic initialization while DINOv3 supplies dense spatial correspondence. The strongest correspondence appears at an intermediate layer: block 18 gives a 4.93x same-region versus cross-region discrimination ratio, compared with 1.48x at the final block. Against CLIP ViT-L/14, DINOv3 shows only a small aggregate advantage but a +16.8 pp gain on anatomical regions, indicating that image-level contrastive supervision captures coarse facial layout but not fine-grained anatomical identity. These results establish frozen DINOv3 as a strong zero-shot representation for region-level facial correspondence and identify intermediate self-supervised features as the most useful layer for dense face analysis.

</details>


### [16] [Cotton-SF YOLO: Learning Structural and Frequency Cues for Early Cotton Square Detection in Complex Field Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14445)
*Chengjia Zhang,Yu Li,Feiri Ali,Yan Zhang,Xin Chen,Longke He,Daokun Ma,Liting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于YOLO26m的Cotton-SF YOLO框架，通过动态蛇形卷积（Dynamic Snake Convolution）增强不规则边缘特征提取，并引入频域特征调制模块（将频谱增强融入C2f结构）以强化判别性边缘/纹理信息、抑制复杂叶片背景干扰，在自制棉铃标注数据集上达到mAP50=0.8196、mAP50:95=0.4942、召回率=0.7939，较基线分别提升1.25%、3.45%、2.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棉铃（cotton squares）是棉花早期生殖生长的重要表型指标，但田间复杂环境下棉铃尺寸小、易被遮挡、模糊、光照多变且与叶片对比度低，现有检测方法对此场景探索不足，亟需针对小目标、弱边缘和复杂背景的专用检测框架。

Method: 以YOLO26m为基线，提出Cotton-SF YOLO：（1）引入动态蛇形卷积（Dynamic Snake Convolution）替代部分标准卷积，自适应提取细长/不规则棉铃边缘的形变特征；（2）设计频域特征调制模块，在C2f结构中融入频谱增强操作，重校准频域表示以强化边缘与纹理线索，同时抑制棉叶背景的干扰。

Result: 在自建田间棉铃数据集上，模型mAP50=0.8196、mAP50:95=0.4942、召回率=0.7939，较基线YOLO26m分别提升1.25%、3.45%、2.96%；消融实验表明结构特征与频域特征具有互补效果。

Conclusion: 所提Cotton-SF YOLO通过动态蛇形卷积与频域特征调制模块的协同作用，有效提升了复杂田间环境下小尺寸棉铃的检测性能，验证了结构感知与频率增强在农业小目标检测中的互补价值。

Abstract: Cotton squares are important phenotypic indicators of the early reproductive growth of cotton, and automatic field detection of cotton squares provides an important basis for cotton growth monitoring and precision cultivation management. However, early cotton square detection in complex field environments remains insufficiently explored, as cotton squares are small, frequently occluded, easily blurred, subject to illumination variations, and exhibit low contrast against surrounding cotton leaves. To address these challenges, we propose a task-oriented framework based on YOLO26m, named Cotton-SF YOLO, for cotton square detection under natural field conditions. To improve the perception of small and irregular cotton square boundaries, we introduce Dynamic Snake Convolution into the detector, enabling adaptive extraction of deformable edge features. Furthermore, a frequency-domain feature modulation module is designed by incorporating spectral enhancement into the C2f structure, which recalibrate frequency-domain representations and strengthen discriminative edge and texture cues while reducing interference from complex cotton leaf backgrounds. Trained and evaluated on our newly constructed and annotated field dataset with manually annotated cotton squares, the proposed model achieves mAP$_{50}$, mAP$_{50:95}$, and recall values of 0.8196, 0.4942, and 0.7939, improving over the baseline YOLO26m by 1.25%, 3.45%, and 2.96%, respectively. Ablation experiments and visualization demonstrate that the best performance is achieved with the complementary effects of structural and frequency cues.

</details>


### [17] [G$^2$SR: Geometric Methods for Fast and Memory-Efficient Gaussian-based Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14470)
*Dasong Gao,Vivienne Sze,Sertac Karaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G2SR是一种面向少视图RGB图像表面重建的高效方法，通过轻量级神经网络前端检测和跟踪2D高斯点云对应关系，再利用多视图几何解析后端三角化为3D高斯点云，在保持高几何精度的同时实现了69-89 FPS的重建速度，GPU内存仅需203 MB，比现有端到端方法快5-107倍，为在线机器人应用提供了实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少视图RGB图像重建3D表面是移动机器人在线探索的关键需求，但现有方法要么基于3D高斯点云(3DGS)的传统光度优化方法存在"漂浮物"伪影，要么端到端方法依赖大型Transformer网络，计算量大、内存占用高且泛化性差。作者希望找到一种既快速又节省内存的解决方案，同时保持几何精度。

Method: G2SR将任务分解为两部分：1）轻量级神经网络前端：在图像平面上检测和跟踪2D高斯点云对应关系；2）解析后端：利用多视图几何原理，将2D对应关系解析三角化为度量尺度的3D高斯点云。这种设计利用了任务中良态的核心——给定跨视图2D splat对应关系，3D splat可以通过几何解析得到。

Result: 在ScanNet、Replica和DTU数据集上，G2SR在几何精度上匹配或超越最先进的端到端方法。在2-3视图输入、384×512分辨率下，重建速度达到69-89 FPS，GPU内存仅203 MB（比端到端方法节省5-107倍）。

Conclusion: G2SR通过分解为轻量级前端和解析后端，成功实现了少视图下的快速、精确、低内存的3D高斯表面重建，为在线机器人应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Few-view surface reconstruction recovers the visible surfaces of a scene from a few posed RGB images, providing the 3D models that robots need to explore and interact online. On mobile platforms, the reconstruction must be fast and geometrically accurate while keeping a small memory footprint to ensure safe and efficient operation. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers a high-fidelity scene representation, but building it from a few views is ill-posed, as many distinct surfaces reproduce the same images, making traditional photometric methods prone to "floater" artifacts. End-to-end methods resolve the ambiguity by regressing splats with large, usually Transformer-based, networks that require heavy compute and memory while generalizing poorly to new scenes. We propose G2SR, which exploits a well-posed core of the task: given cross-view 2D splat correspondences, 3D splats follow analytically from multi-view geometry. G2SR employs a lightweight neural frontend to detect and track 2D Gaussian splats on the image plane and an analytic backend to triangulate each into a metric-scale 3D splat. On ScanNet, Replica, and DTU, G2SR matches or exceeds the geometric accuracy of state-of-the-art end-to-end methods while running at 69-89 reconstructions per second within 203 MB of GPU memory (5-107x less) for 2- and 3-view inputs at 384 x 512 resolution, offering a practical path to online Gaussian-based surface reconstruction.

</details>


### [18] [Immediate 3D Gaussian Splat Reconstruction of Unordered Input with Global Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14481)
*Andreas Meuleman,Linus Franke,Boris Zhestiankin,Camille Montemagni,George Drettakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的即时反馈重建方法，能够处理乱序图像序列，提供全局一致性的辐射场重建。通过利用视觉地点识别模型和共视图进行快速匹配，高效选择关键帧，结合GPU优化和高斯原语放置实现快速局部重建；引入基于聚类的闭环方法处理乱序输入；并采用渐进层次结构扩展到大型场景。实验表明该方法在多个数据集上实现了高视觉质量的即时反馈3DGS重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS重建方法要么需要离线处理所有图像（如SfM），计算成本高且无法即时反馈；要么是增量式方法（如SLAM）只能处理顺序输入，无法应对乱序采集场景。本文旨在提供一种既能处理乱序图像序列又能即时反馈的全局一致3DGS重建方案。

Method: 1) 利用视觉地点识别模型和共视图对乱序图像进行快速匹配；2) 通过共视图高效选择高连通性关键帧，提升有序序列的重建质量；3) 结合GPU优化和精细的高斯原语放置实现快速局部重建；4) 提出基于聚类的闭环方法，利用共视图实现无需顺序输入的闭环检测；5) 引入渐进层次结构，使方法可扩展至大型场景。

Result: 在多个数据集上（包含数千张输入图像）实现了即时反馈的3DGS重建，并取得了良好的视觉质量。该方法能够处理乱序图像序列，同时保持全局一致性，且效率优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文首次提出了针对辐射场采集的即时反馈解决方案，能够在乱序图像序列中实现全局一致的3DGS重建。通过快速匹配、高效关键帧选择、基于聚类的闭环和渐进层次结构，方法在保证视觉质量的同时支持大规模场景，为实时3D重建提供了新途径。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the method of choice for reconstructing and real-time rendering of captured scenes. To capture a scene with good visual quality, continuous image sequences are usually combined with out-of-order shots for better scene coverage. Structure from motion can reconstruct such captures, but only after they are all available and often with high computational cost. Incremental reconstruction methods -- often derived from SLAM solutions -- provide immediate feedback, but cannot handle the out-of-order capture we require. We provide the first immediate feedback solution for such radiance field capture that provides global consistency. We first introduce a method for fast matching in out-of-order sequences, by repurposing visual place recognition models and a covisibility graph, and provide an efficient way to find highly connected keyframes, improving quality even for ordered sequences. We show how these steps -- together with GPU optimization and careful Gaussian primitive placement -- provide fast local reconstruction, in our challenging radiance field reconstruction case. We then introduce a novel cluster-based method, again using the covisibility graph, to provide efficient loop closure that does not require sequential input. Finally, to handle large scenes in our context, we introduce a progressive hierarchy that allows our method to scale to large environments, without compromising efficiency. Our results show we provide immediate feedback 3DGS reconstruction with good visual quality in several datasets, with up to thousands of input images.

</details>


### [19] [Reinforcing Egocentric Spatial Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models via Ego Scene Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14497)
*Chi Kit Wong,Ye Pan,Yuanhuiyi Lyu,Xu Zheng,Zidong Cao,Lutao Jiang,Zixin Zhang,Huiyu Zhou,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ego Scene Augmentation (ESA)框架，通过Ego-element Graph增强多模态大模型在自我中心场景中的空间感知能力，在EgoTextVQA基准上室内场景提升8.14%，室外场景提升8.72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在复杂自我中心场景中因空间感知能力有限而难以有效进行空间推理，亟需提升其从自我中心视角感知空间的能力。

Method: 1) 构建Ego-element Graph，利用视觉基础模型封装和整合自我中心空间特征；2) 通过该图增强MLLMs对自我中心场景的空间感知能力。

Result: 在EgoTextVQA基准上，室内场景准确率提升8.14%，室外场景提升8.72%；室内购物子集上表现最为突出。

Conclusion: ESA框架有效提升了自我中心VQA中的空间感知能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Egocentric Visual Question Answering (VQA) has attracted widespread attention as an important task for enabling Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to interact with the real world. However, existing MLLMs struggle to perform effective spatial reasoning in complex egocentric scenes due to their limited spatial perception capabilities. To this end, we introduce Ego Scene Augmentation (ESA), an egocentric spatial perception framework, which actively enhances the spatial perception capabilities from the egocentric perspective, powered by the proposed Ego-element Graph. Our core insight is leveraging the Ego-element Graph as an intermediary representation to augment the egocentric spatial perception of MLLMs via visual foundational models. Specifically, we 1) construct the Ego-element Graph, which encapsulates and integrates egocentric spatial features enabled by visual foundational models; 2) enhance the spatial perception capabilities of MLLMs via the Ego-element Graph for ego-perspective scenes. Our proposed ESA framework presents significant performance improvement on the EgoTextVQA benchmark. We achieve an 8.14% gain on the indoor setting and an 8.72% gain on the outdoor setting. Furthermore, our ESA shows the most impressive performance improvement in the shopping subset of the indoor setting. The project code is publicly available.

</details>


### [20] [Multi-Scale ViT Inference with Habitat-Fit Priors and kNN Retrieval for Multi-Species Plant Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14509)
*Alper Erten,Murilo Gustineli,Adrian Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DS@GT ARC团队在PlantCLEF 2026挑战赛中获得第三名的解决方案，该方案针对植被样方图像中的多物种植物识别问题，使用基于DINOv2 ViT-L/14的多尺度图块分类器，结合FAISS kNN检索、时间融合、栖息地适宜性降权和地理掩码，在仅使用单标签图像训练的条件下，实现了private-leaderboard上0.43902的macro-F1（第三名）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决植被样方照片中多物种植物识别的实际需求——系统需要从高分辨率（约3000×3000像素）样方图像中预测所有存在的物种，但训练数据仅包含单标签的个体植物图像，这带来了训练-测试分布不匹配的挑战。

Method: 1) 多尺度图块分解：将每张样方图像分解为多尺度图块，使用微调的DINOv2 ViT-L/14分类器对每个图块进行预测；2) 混合检索：将图块级预测与FAISS kNN检索器的结果融合；3) 后处理：包括源感知时间融合（对重复访问的样方进行融合）、栖息地适宜性降权（注入地理和海拔先验）以及西南欧地理掩码；4) 探索了交叉区域transformer+噪声学生蒸馏、标签作为查询的transformer解码器、实例感知分割裁剪等方向但未提升性能。

Result: 最终提交在private-leaderboard上达到macro-F1 0.43902（第三名），public leaderboard上为0.51096；同一管线的未选择配置在private集上超过0.45。消融实验显示栖息地适宜性降权和多尺度聚合是最大的贡献因素。

Conclusion: 基于DINOv2的多尺度图块分类+检索混合方法在PlantCLEF 2026竞赛中取得了有效结果，但一些更复杂的训练方法（交叉区域transformer、标签查询解码器、实例分割）未能带来提升，表明该任务中简单而精心设计的推理管线可能比复杂的训练策略更有效。

Abstract: This paper describes DS@GT ARC's third-place solution to the PlantCLEF 2026 challenge on multi-species plant identification in vegetation quadrat images, where systems must predict every species present in high-resolution (~3000 x 3000 pixel) plot photographs while training only on single-label images of individual plants. The pipeline is built around a fine-tuned DINOv2 ViT-L/14 classifier applied over a multi-scale tile decomposition of each quadrat, with per-tile predictions blended with a FAISS kNN retriever and post-processed by source-aware temporal fusion across repeated plot visits, a habitat-fit demotion that injects geographic and altitude priors from the training data, and a South-Western Europe geographic mask. Habitat-fit demotion and multi-scale aggregation are the largest individual contributors in the ablations. Two complementary training-centric directions, a cross-region transformer with noisy-student distillation on the LUCAS dataset and a label-as-query transformer decoder over synthetic CLS-domain pseudo-quadrats, yielded null results. An inference-time augmentation with instance-aware segmentation crops also did not improve performance. The selected submission reaches a private-leaderboard macro-F1 of 0.43902 (third place; public 0.51096); an unselected configuration of the same pipeline scored above 0.45 on the private set. Code: https://github.com/dsgt-arc/plantclef-2026.

</details>


### [21] [Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting Data Using GPU-friendly Graphics Texture Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14513)
*Amir Said,Randall Rauwendaal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用GPU硬件加速的纹理压缩方案（BC1/BC7）来压缩3D高斯溅射中的球谐颜色系数，通过局部分组和重排实现高效并行解码，在保持可接受的视觉质量的同时显著降低内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯溅射(3DGS)使用球谐函数(SH)建模视角相关颜色，导致每个基元需要大量SH系数，内存需求大。现有压缩方法未充分利用现代GPU的并行解码能力，因此需要一种能兼顾压缩率与GPU并行渲染效率的方法。

Method: 采用GPU硬件加速的纹理压缩格式（BC1和BC7）压缩SH颜色系数，通过将基元按颜色局部分组和重新排序，比直接使用2D纹理压缩更高效；并引入保持随机访问的码率控制策略，支持大规模并行化而不牺牲渲染性能。

Result: 基于BC1和BC7纹理压缩格式的实验表明，GPU解压缩带来的渲染质量下降可忽略不计或几乎不可察觉，同时实现了有效的内存压缩。

Conclusion: 所提出的GPU友好型SH系数压缩方法在保持高质量渲染的同时显著降低内存占用，且利用了现代GPU的硬件加速纹理解码能力，为3DGS场景的内存效率优化提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Techniques for modeling 3D scenes from image collections, such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), are capable of generating high-quality novel views by leveraging graphics primitives with view-dependent appearance. In 3DGS, spherical harmonic (SH) are employed to model view-dependent color, resulting in a large number of SH coefficients per primitive and large memory requirements. While compression approaches have been proposed to mitigate this problem, they do not exploit the capabilities of modern Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) for parallel decoding and rendering. In this paper, we propose a method for compressing SH color coefficients using texture compression schemes specifically designed for efficient parallel GPU decoding and supported by dedicated hardware acceleration. It is shown that those methods can compress color coefficients more effectively than 2D textures by exploiting the fact that primitives can be locally grouped and reordered according to color. Furthermore, we introduce a bit-rate control strategy that preserves random access, enabling large-scale parallelization without compromising rendering performance. Experimental results using BC1 and BC7 texture compression formats show that GPU-based decompression can be achieved with negligible or imperceptible degradation in the visual quality of rendered 3DGS scenes.

</details>


### [22] [VTM-Nav: Hierarchical Visual-Topological Memory for Cross-Episode Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14514)
*Xiaoran Xu,Yupeng Wu,Tianyu Xue,Yifan Xu,Xuanran Dong,Xiaoshan Yang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨场景目标导航任务，并设计了基于VLM的免训练导航框架VTM，通过分层视觉-拓扑记忆实现经验复用，在多个基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标导航方法在场景间重置智能体状态，无法利用跨场景经验。本文旨在探索智能体在同一场景中反复运行、仅保留自获取经验并固定模型参数时，如何有效复用经验以提升导航效率。

Method: 提出VTM框架：1）分层视觉-拓扑记忆（房间级+物体级）组织场景知识；2）从粗到细的匹配机制检索相关经验；3）经验仅在当前观测一致时作为软引导；4）保守执行守卫缓解振荡、阻塞和过早停止问题。

Result: 在HM3D v0.1、HM3D v0.2和MP3D三个基准上，VTM均超越增强版WMNav基线（具备跨场景文本记忆），取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉-拓扑经验复用在不同数据集上展示出有效性和鲁棒性，验证了跨场景经验复用的价值。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation requires an embodied agent to locate and reach an instance of a specified object category in an indoor environment. Recent training-free approaches leverage vision-language models (VLMs) for open-vocabulary semantic reasoning, but are typically evaluated under an episodic protocol that resets all scene-specific state after each episode. We introduce Cross-Episode Object-Goal Navigation, in which an agent repeatedly operates in the same scene, retains only self-acquired experience, and keeps its model parameters fixed. To support experience reuse, we present \method, a training-free VLM navigation framework with a persistent hierarchical Visual-Topological Memory (VTM). The VTM organizes scene knowledge at room and object levels and retrieves relevant experience through coarse-to-fine matching, providing memory as soft guidance only when it agrees with current observations. A conservative execution guard further mitigates oscillations, blocked motions, and premature stopping. Under a controlled same-scene protocol, we evaluate \method{} on three benchmarks, HM3D v0.1, HM3D v0.2, and MP3D, and compare it with a strengthened WMNav baseline augmented with cross-episode textual memory, while keeping the VLM backbone and action pipeline identical. \method{} achieves the best performance across all three benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness and robustness of structured visual-topological experience reuse across datasets.

</details>


### [23] [Uni-AdaVD: Universal Concept Erasure for Visual Generation via Orthogonal Value Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14521)
*Qifan Zhou,Yuan Wang,Yanbin Hao,Xiang Wang,Kuien Liu,Richang Hong,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Uni-AdaVD 是一个通用的推理时概念擦除框架，通过将多模态注意力的值空间作为统一干预空间，结合编码器感知的目标表示构建、正交值分解和自适应擦除偏移，在不修改原始模型权重的情况下，有效移除目标概念并保留非目标先验，在多种视觉生成架构（U-Net、DiT、自回归图像生成器、文本到视频模型）上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成模型从非精选预训练数据中不可避免地吸收了不良概念，现有擦除方法通常受限于特定架构，难以在移除目标概念的同时保留非目标内容和生成先验。

Method: Uni-AdaVD 将多模态注意力的值空间作为统一干预空间，利用编码器感知的目标表示构建定位异构文本编码器中的目标语义，结合正交值分解与自适应擦除偏移来压制目标语义方向，无需更新原始模型权重。

Result: 在 U-Net、DiT、自回归图像生成器以及文本到视频模型上的大量实验表明，Uni-AdaVD 在单概念和多概念擦除上均表现强劲，同时有效保留了非目标先验。

Conclusion: Uni-AdaVD 为现代视觉生成模型提供了一种高效且可适应的安全机制，其代码已开源。

Abstract: Visual generative models inevitably absorb undesirable concepts from uncurated pretraining data, making concept erasure essential for safe deployment. Existing erasure methods, however, are often architecture-specific and struggle to remove target concepts while preserving non-target content and generative priors. We present Uni-AdaVD, a universal inference-time concept erasure framework for visual generation. Uni-AdaVD treats the value space of multimodal attention as a unified intervention space and introduces encoder-aware target representation construction to localize target semantics across heterogeneous text encoders. It further combines orthogonal value decomposition with an adaptive erasing shift to suppress target semantic directions without updating the original model weights. Extensive experiments on U-Net-, DiT-, and autoregressive image generators, as well as text-to-video models, demonstrate strong performance on single- and multi-concept erasure while preserving non-target priors. These results suggest that Uni-AdaVD provides an efficient and adaptable safety mechanism for modern visual generative models. Our code is available at https://github.com/QifanZhou/Uni-AdaVD.

</details>


### [24] [SwinAD: Multi-stage feature reconstruction for unsupervised industrial anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14534)
*Huong Ninh,Chien Thai,Mai Xuan Trang,Vu-Minh Le,Thanh Ha Le,Long Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SwinAD，一种基于重建的多类无监督异常检测框架，利用冻结的预训练Swin Transformer V2编码器和特征多样性保留重建解码器，通过层级特征、逐阶段瓶颈模块和双分支重建机制，在MVTec AD、VisA和Real-IAD上实现competitive的图像级性能和强大的像素级定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督异常检测方法主要在单类场景下表现良好，但在多类场景中面对多样化的正常模式容易过度泛化，降低了区分正常与异常区域的能力。需要一种能有效处理多类无监督异常检测的框架。

Method: 采用冻结的预训练Swin Transformer V2作为层级编码器，提取语义丰富的多尺度特征；使用带有Dropout的逐阶段瓶颈模块防止恒等映射，鼓励对正常模式的鲁棒重建；提出特征多样性保留重建框架，通过双分支解码保持互补的重建假设而非单一解码分支；将编码器特征与两种重建特征的差异跨多尺度聚合生成最终异常图。

Result: 在MVTec AD、VisA和Real-IAD三个工业异常检测基准上，SwinAD取得了competitive的图像级性能和强大的像素级定位精度，尤其在MVTec AD上像素级AP和1有显著提升。

Conclusion: 将层级Swin特征与多样性多尺度重建相结合，能显著提升多类无监督异常检测场景中的像素级定位能力。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection aims to identify and localize defective regions without relying on exhaustive annotations of all possible defect types. Although recent unsupervised methods have achieved strong performance, most are primarily designed for single-class settings and often struggle in multi-class scenarios, where diverse normal patterns may lead to over-generalization and reduce the discriminative capability between normal and anomalous regions. In this paper, we propose SwinAD, a reconstruction-based framework for multi-class unsupervised anomaly detection that leverages a frozen pretrained Swin Transformer V2 encoder and a feature diversity-preserving reconstruction decoder. The hierarchical encoder provides semantically rich multi-scale features, while stage-wise bottleneck modules with dropout prevent trivial identity mapping and encourage robust reconstruction of normal patterns. To further improve localization, we introduce a feature diversity-preserving reconstruction framework that maintains complementary reconstruction hypotheses instead of relying on a single decoding branch. The discrepancies between encoder features and the two reconstructed features are then aggregated across multiple scales to produce the final anomaly map. Experiments conducted on three industrial anomaly detection benchmarks, including MVTec AD, VisA, and Real-IAD, demonstrate that SwinAD achieves competitive image-level performance and strong pixel-level localization accuracy, with particularly notable improvements in pixel-level AP and 1 on MVTec AD. These results indicate that combining hierarchical Swin features with diverse multi-scale reconstruction substantially improve pixel-level localization in multi-class unsupervised anomaly setting.

</details>


### [25] [3D Geometric Tooth Alignment Planning via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14544)
*Yong Li,Jianwen Lou,Jiayue Ma,Yao-Xiang Ding,Youyi Zheng,Haihua Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于深度强化学习（DRL）的框架，用于自动生成3D牙齿正畸对齐路径，通过将规划过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程，并利用改进的DDPG算法（集成Transformer智能体、动态掩码方案和两阶段课程学习）在10K专家数据集上实现了优于现有基线的路径安全性和几何效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正畸对齐规划是数字正畸的核心，但传统方法依赖人工设计或简单优化，难以高效处理高维状态空间和复杂空间约束（如碰撞避免、路径效率），亟需自动化智能规划方法。

Method: 将规划过程建模为MDP，基于DDPG算法进行强化学习，包含三项创新：(1) Transformer智能体建模牙齿间复杂空间交互；(2) 动态掩码机制每步仅移动稀疏子集牙齿，模拟临床顺序对齐逻辑；(3) 两阶段课程学习逐步增加任务难度以保证训练稳定性。

Result: 在包含10K个专家设计治疗计划的数据集上评估，所提方法在路径安全性和几何效率方面优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 该DRL框架提供了一种鲁棒且自动化的3D几何正畸对齐规划解决方案，能有效优化几何轨迹并整合必要空间约束。

Abstract: 3D geometric tooth alignment planning, which determines sequential trajectories from initial malocclusion to the final target alignment, is a cornerstone of modern digital orthodontics. This paper presents a novel deep reinforcement learning (DRL) framework to automate the generation of these alignment paths. We formulate the planning process as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) to capture its sequential decision-making nature, focusing on optimizing geometric trajectories while integrating essential spatial constraints, such as inter-dental collision avoidance and path efficiency. The proposed method leverages the Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) algorithm, enhanced by three key innovations: (1) a Transformer-based agent to model complex spatial interactions between teeth and manage high-dimensional state-action spaces; (2) a dynamic masking scheme that restricts movement to a sparse subset of teeth per step, better reflecting the clinical logic of sequential alignment; and (3) a two-stage curriculum learning strategy that gradually increases task difficulty to ensure training stability and efficient path discovery. We evaluate our approach on a dataset of 10K expert-designed treatment plans based on clinical data. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms existing baselines in terms of path safety and geometric efficiency, providing a robust and automated solution for 3D geometric orthodontic alignment planning.

</details>


### [26] [AdaTurn: Budget-Aware Test-Time Scaling for Active Visual Perception Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14547)
*Susan Liang,Chao Huang,Filippos Bellos,Jing Bi,Jason J Corso,Chenliang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaTurn 是一个预算感知框架，通过条件化智能体于允许的交互轮次并显式训练边界行为，解决了主动视觉智能体在部署时因预算不足而导致的灾难性截断问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将交互预算视为隐藏变量，当实际预算小于智能体偏好的轨迹长度时，交互往往在被截断前未产生有效答案，导致“灾难性截断”。需要一种预算感知的方法来适应不同部署预算。

Method: 提出 AdaTurn 框架，核心组件为 Forced-Answer DAPO (FA-DAPO)，将超预算事件从遮掩或惩罚的失败转化为可训练的最终决策步骤，教会模型在无法继续使用工具时综合部分证据。此外，在训练和推理时随机化预算，并引入负载均衡调度器使操作实用。

Result: 显著提升低预算下的准确率，例如 VisualProbe-Medium 在 4 轮交互下从 36.7% 提升至 47.6%；同时保持大预算下的强扩展性，并能有效迁移到多种骨干网络和通用多模态基准。

Conclusion: AdaTurn 有效克服了灾难性截断问题，使主动视觉智能体能够稳健地处理可变部署预算，兼顾低预算效率与高预算性能。

Abstract: Active visual agents solve fine-grained image tasks by interleaving reasoning with image-grounding actions across multiple turns. However, deployment-time rollout budgets are rarely fixed: some requests permit long rollouts, while others require the agent to act under a tight turn limit. Existing methods train the policy as if the rollout budget were hidden, so when the available budget is smaller than the trajectory the agent prefers, the interaction is often truncated before any valid answer is produced; we term this failure \emph{catastrophic truncation}. To overcome this challenge, we present AdaTurn, a budget-aware framework that conditions the agent on the allowed number of turns and explicitly trains the boundary behavior induced by the budget. Our key component, Forced-Answer DAPO (FA-DAPO), converts the over-budget event from a masked or penalized failure into a trainable final-decision step, teaching the model to synthesize partial evidence when further tool use is no longer possible. We further randomize rollout budgets during both training and inference and introduce a load-balanced scheduler that makes such operations practical. AdaTurn substantially improves low-budget accuracy, for example raising VisualProbe-Medium from 36.7% to 47.6% at four turns, while preserving strong scaling at larger budgets and transferring effectively to multiple backbones and general multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [27] [HyMobileAgent: Data-Environment Co-Scaling for Efficient GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14548)
*Hy Vision Team,Huawen Shen,Zhengyang Tang,Shangpin Peng,Liang Wu,Anran Zhang,Weinong Wang,Yiduo Guo,Chenxin Li,Zhengyao Fang,Yang Ding,Junyi Li,Fei Tang,Zheng Ruan,Yi Zhang,Xingran Zhou,Dingchen Yang,Sunqi Fan,Zhiyi Wan,Han Hu,Xin Lai,Pengyuan Lyu,Chengquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyMobileAgent 是一个基于 Hy3.0-VL-A3B 视觉原生基础模型的移动 GUI 智能体，通过联合数据与环境中心缩放框架、渐进式训练方案（中期训练、监督微调、强化学习）及多项创新技术（GUI 感知飞轮、知识管道、百万级动作数据管道、PhoneWorld Mock App Factory、规划-反射机制），在无需依赖极端模型缩放的前提下，有效应对移动智能体在精细感知、高质量数据获取和长程稳健决策三方面的耦合挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动 GUI 智能体面临三个耦合约束：复杂界面的精细感知、高质量交互数据的可扩展获取、以及复合执行错误下的稳健长程决策。现有方法过度依赖模型缩放，而忽视数据和环境驱动的瓶颈突破，亟需一种联合数据与环境中心缩放的框架来解决这些关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出了 HyMobileAgent，核心方法包括：(1) 基于 Hy3.0-VL-A3B 视觉原生基础模型（原生任意分辨率输入、A3B 级部署预算、32K 上下文窗口）；(2) 联合数据与环境中心缩放框架：GUI 感知飞轮（模拟界面合成+拒绝采样+图标增强）、知识管道（教程视频→结构化交互数据）、百万级动作数据管道（2000+沙箱/真机实例+自动故障归因）、PhoneWorld Mock App Factory（34 个模拟应用、34000+任务）；(3) 规划-反射机制与显式死循环检测；(4) 渐进式训练方案：中期训练→监督微调→任务特定奖励的强化学习。

Result: 通过联合数据与环境中心缩放框架，HyMobileAgent 在移动 GUI 智能体的精细感知、高质量数据获取和长程稳健决策三个关键维度上取得显著突破，在不依赖极端模型缩放的情况下实现了强大的移动交互能力。

Conclusion: 移动 GUI 智能体的发展不应仅依赖模型缩放，而应通过数据与环境中心的联合缩放框架系统性地解决感知、数据和决策三大瓶颈。HyMobileAgent 展示了视觉原生基础模型结合精心设计的数据管道、训练环境和渐进式训练方案的有效性，为移动端数字具身智能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: As large multimodal models move from understanding content to operating on digital environments, mobile GUI has emerged as a challenging and consequential testbed for digital embodied intelligence. Mobile agents operate under three coupled constraints: precise perception of complex interfaces, scalable acquisition of high-quality interaction data, and robust long-horizon decision making under compounding execution errors. This report presents HyMobileAgent, a mobile GUI agent built on Hy3.0-VL-A3B, a vision-native foundation model featuring native any-resolution input, an A3B-scale deployment budget, and a 32K context window to model extended interaction histories. Rather than relying solely on model scaling, we develop a joint data and environment centric scaling framework to address the key bottlenecks of mobile interaction.
  Our framework integrates a GUI perception flywheel combining mock-interface synthesis, rejection sampling, and icon-specific augmentation; a knowledge pipeline that transforms tutorial videos into structured interaction data; a million-scale action data pipeline deployed across more than 2000 sandbox and real-device instances with automated failure attribution; the PhoneWorld Mock App Factory, providing a resettable training environment with 34 mock applications and over 34000 tasks; and a structured Planning-and-Reflection mechanism with explicit dead-loop detection for reliable long-horizon execution.
  We also introduce a progressive training recipe consisting of mid-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning with task-specific reward designs.

</details>


### [28] [Breaking the Model Forgetting Cycle in Long-Incremental 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14560)
*Peisheng Qian,Jie Xu,Xulei Yang,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LDMR框架，通过学习动态驱动的记忆与回顾机制，解决长序列增量3D目标检测中的模型遗忘问题，在SUN RGB-D和ScanNetV2上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于伪标签的增量3D目标检测方法在短增量阶段表现尚可，但在长增量序列中遭受严重模型遗忘。作者发现一种有害的自我强化循环：新类别的数据分布偏移导致旧类别遗忘，进而产生伪标签的累积误差，加剧模型退化。

Method: 提出LDMR框架，包含两个核心机制：(i) 类人式的阶段内回顾——将每个增量阶段划分为多个子阶段训练，专注于记忆最易遗忘的物体；(ii) 场景感知的跨阶段记忆进化——通过联合考虑场景可学习性和多样性，演化记忆库以在连续两个阶段间传递知识。该框架通过监测周期性训练检查点上的每类检测质量来驱动这些机制。

Result: 在室内基准SUN RGB-D和ScanNetV2上的多个长增量协议实验中，LDMR显著缓解了模型遗忘，并以明显优势超越所有基线方法。

Conclusion: LDMR框架通过模拟人类学习过程中的记忆与回顾机制，有效打破了增量检测中的自我强化遗忘循环，为长序列增量3D目标检测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Incremental 3D object detection requires a detector to learn novel object classes while remembering previously learned ones over sequentially arriving data. Previous methods, primarily based on pseudo-labeling, perform reasonably in short-incremental stages but still suffer from severe model forgetting when dealing with long-incremental sequences. We investigate this failure and reveal a detrimental self-reinforcing cycle: data distribution shift of novel classes causes model forgetting on old classes, which further produces accumulated error in pseudo-labeling that exacerbates model degradation. To address this issue, we draw inspiration from the human learning process and propose the \emph{Learning-Dynamics-driven Memory and Review} (LDMR) framework. LDMR monitors per-class detection quality at periodic training checkpoints and uses these learning-dynamics signals to drive two innovative mechanisms, namely (i) human-like intra-stage review that divides each incremental stage into multiple sub-stages' training and concentrates on remembering the most-forgotten objects, and (ii) scene-aware cross-stage memory evolution that evolves a memory bank to transfer knowledge between two consecutive stages by jointly considering scene learnability and diversity. Extensive experiments across multiple long-incremental protocols on indoor benchmarks SUN RGB-D and ScanNetV2 show that LDMR substantially mitigates the model forgetting and outperforms all baselines by a clear margin. Code is available at https://github.com/qianpeisheng/LDMR.

</details>


### [29] [Advanced Image Generation: Negative Prompt Optimization and Latent Classifier Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14580)
*Vaddi Charan Sai Nandan Reddy,Harini B,Chandana M S*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合负提示优化（基于微调序列到序列LLM）和潜在空间分类器引导的双重引导框架，用于改进Stable Diffusion图像生成质量。系统自动生成优化负提示，并通过CNN-RNN混合分类器评估和引导扩散步骤，回滚低质量潜在更新。实验表明该方法减少了伪影并提升了语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stable Diffusion生成的图像常包含伪影或语义偏差，现有方法缺乏对生成过程的自动引导与质量评估机制，需要一种能够自动优化提示词并在扩散过程中动态纠正低质量生成步骤的方法。

Method: 采用双引导框架：(1) 微调序列到序列LLM自动生成优化负提示，用于在扩散过程中抑制不期望的特征；(2) 训练一个CNN-RNN混合分类器在潜在空间中对扩散步进行质量评估，遇到低质量预测时回滚潜在更新，仅保留高质量生成路径。

Result: 与基线扩散模型相比，该方法能有效减少生成图像中的伪影，并提高语义保真度，验证了双重引导机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 负提示优化与潜在空间分类器引导的组合能够显著改善文本到图像生成的质量，为扩散模型的自动质量控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present a novel system that integrates negative prompt optimization via a fine-tuned sequence-to-sequence LLM and latent-space classifier guidance to improve the quality of images generated by Stable Diffusion. Our approach automatically generates optimized negative prompts, and employs a CNN-RNN hybrid classifier to evaluate and guide diffusion steps, rolling back low-quality latent updates. Experimental results demonstrate that our dual-guidance framework reduces artifacts and improves semantic fidelity compared to baseline diffusion.

</details>


### [30] [MagicPrompt: Ultra-Lightweight Prompt Tuning for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14595)
*Yinhan Zhang,Dinwei Tan,Xianghao Kong,Yue Ma,Yeying Jin,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MagicPrompt 是一种轻量级框架，通过 Attention-Embedded Prompt Tuning 和 Dual-Space Reward Feedback Optimization，在大规模视频扩散模型上仅需不到 1% 的可训练参数即可达到竞争性能，显著降低了训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视频扩散模型全量微调计算成本极高，现有参数高效微调方法在十亿级模型上仍需大量可训练参数，且基于奖励的训练在条件引导任务中面临噪声引起的优化不稳定问题。

Method: MagicPrompt 首先采用 Attention-Embedded Prompt Tuning，通过轻量级软提示以极少的参数引导生成并保留预训练知识；再引入 Dual-Space Reward Feedback Optimization，利用自监督潜在目标改进条件引导的奖励训练。

Result: MagicPrompt 在不到 1% 的可训练参数下达到竞争性能，并显著降低训练成本。

Conclusion: MagicPrompt 证明了通过注意力嵌入提示调优和双空间奖励反馈优化相结合，可以在十亿级视频扩散模型上实现极端参数效率和稳定的奖励优化。

Abstract: Large-scale video diffusion models (VDMs) deliver strong generation performance, but full fine-tuning for downstream tasks incurs prohibitive computational costs. Existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods have two critical flaws on billion-scale models: they still require substantial trainable parameters, and reward-based training suffers from noise-induced optimization instability in condition-guided tasks. We propose MagicPrompt, a lightweight framework that achieves extreme parameter efficiency and stable reward optimization. It first adopts Attention-Embedded Prompt Tuning, which steers generation via lightweight soft prompts with orders of magnitude fewer parameters while preserving pre-trained knowledge. It further introduces Dual-Space Reward Feedback Optimization, which uses self-supervised latent objectives to improve condition-guided reward training. Experiments show MagicPrompt reaches competitive performance with less than 1\% trainable parameters and notably reduces training costs.

</details>


### [31] [Hough-SIFT: Robust Image Registration for Linear Structures via Hough Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14598)
*Masaki Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Hough-SIFT方法，在霍夫空间中进行SIFT描述子匹配，以解决强线性结构场景下SIFT失效的问题，在保持正常场景精度的同时提升线性场景鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIFT在强线性结构场景（如快门）中因局部特征模糊而经常失效，需要一种能处理此类场景的鲁棒配准方法。

Method: 将SIFT描述子匹配转换到霍夫空间中进行，利用线性结构在霍夫空间形成独特峰值的特点恢复描述子的可区分性。

Result: 实验表明Hough-SIFT在SIFT经常失败的线性场景中表现鲁棒，同时在正常场景中保持与SIFT相当的精度。

Conclusion: Hough-SIFT是一种鲁棒的配准方法，在线性场景下有效克服了SIFT的局限性，且不牺牲正常场景的性能。

Abstract: Image registration is essential in applications such as electronic image stabilization. Scale-Invariant Feature Transform (SIFT), a widely used local keypoint detector and descriptor, typically provides accurate registration; however, it often fails in scenes with strong linear structures (e.g., shutters), where local features become ambiguous. We propose Hough-SIFT, a robust registration method that performs SIFT descriptor matching in Hough space. In this domain, linear structures form distinctive peaks that restore descriptor discriminability. Experiments demonstrate that Hough-SIFT is robust in linear scenes where SIFT frequently fails, while maintaining accuracy comparable to SIFT in normal scenes.

</details>


### [32] [Knowing You at First Glance: Inferring Apparent Personality from Faces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14631)
*Shuhuan Chen,Xiangyu Zhu,Weisong Zhao,Haichao Shi,Xiao-Yu Zhang,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GlanceFace是一个端到端框架，利用视觉-语言模型和语义增强的面部表征模块，从面部图像推断MBTI人格类型，采用不确定性感知学习策略处理噪声标签，在MBTI显性人格基准上表现优异，可用于人机交互中自适应初始交互策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注大五人格模型，且多依赖语言或多模态输入，不清楚仅凭面部线索能否支持有意义的人格特质感知。MBTI人格类型在实践中广泛使用且更易被大语言模型解释，因此探索仅从面部图像推断MBTI人格的可行性。

Method: 提出GlanceFace框架，包含三个核心组件：1）利用视觉-语言模型引入语义先验；2）语义增强的面部表征模块捕捉微小人脸相关线索；3）不确定性感知学习策略处理噪声和主观标注。

Result: 在MBTI显性人格基准上取得优异性能，揭示了面部特征与感知人格特质之间的关系，展示了支持自适应初始交互策略的潜力。

Conclusion: GlanceFace有效证明了仅凭面部图像即可推断MBTI人格类型，为人机交互中机器人快速建立初步交互策略提供了可行方案，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Inferring apparent personality from facial images is important in social scenarios for embodied agents in human-robot interaction. Unlike inferring intrinsic personality traits via conversation, this task models first-impression personality perception based solely on facial appearance before interaction begins. Existing studies mainly focus on the Big Five personality model and often rely on language or multimodal inputs. As a result, it remains unclear whether facial cues alone can support meaningful associations with perceived personality traits. This question is particularly relevant for MBTI types, which are widely used in practice and more readily interpretable by large language models. To this end, we propose \textbf{GlanceFace}, an end-to-end framework for apparent personality inference leveraging vision-language models to introduce semantic priors and a semantic-enhanced facial representation module to capture subtle personality-related cues, together with an uncertainty-aware learning strategy to handle noisy and subjective annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong performance on MBTI-based apparent personality benchmarks and reveal relationships between facial characteristics and perceived personality traits, highlighting its potential to support adaptive initial interaction strategies for embodied agents. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/MrHuan3/GlanceFace.

</details>


### [33] [Autoregressive Modeling of Film with Applications in Video Montage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14645)
*Marcelo Sandoval-Castañeda,Fabian Caba Heilbron,Shiry Ginosar,Bryan Rusell,Josef Sivic,Alexei A. Efros,Greg Shakhnarovich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FilmGPT是一个自回归Transformer模型，通过在大规模电影语料上学习长上下文序列，隐式捕获电影剪辑的“语法”，并在推理时使用镜头约束解码算法从原始素材中选择最佳镜头，无需生成新帧，从而将原始视频素材自动剪辑为连贯的电影序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频蒙太奇（将原始、“不可看”的素材剪辑为连贯电影序列）是一项挑战。传统方法依赖手工编码规则，而本文受大型语言模型启发，希望直接从数据中隐式学习电影的“语法”，避免手工规则。

Method: FilmGPT是一个长上下文自回归Transformer，在大规模电影语料上训练。推理时，采用镜头约束解码算法，从输入的原始素材中选择符合电影统计模式的最佳下一个镜头。模型不生成新视频帧，仅进行镜头选择与排序。

Result: 在镜头序列排序的标准基准上，FilmGPT的下一镜头预测超越了先前最先进水平。用户研究表明，基于FilmGPT的编辑显著优于先前方法。此外，FilmGPT可应用于视频片段自动裁剪、人机协作电影编辑等多种视频蒙太奇任务。

Conclusion: FilmGPT成功地从电影数据中学习了剪辑语法，能够有效将原始素材剪辑为连贯的影片，并在多个任务上取得优异表现，展示了自回归模型在视频编辑领域的潜力。

Abstract: This work introduces FilmGPT, an autoregressive transformer designed to address the challenge of video montage--turning a collection of raw, "unwatchable" footage into coherent cinematic sequences. Inspired by language learning in modern LLMs, we train a long-context autoregressive transformer on a large corpus of movies. The aim is to implicitly capture the "grammar" of film directly from data rather than from hand-coded rules. Unlike other generative models, FilmGPT does not generate any new video frames. Instead, at inference time, we introduce a footage-constrained decoding algorithm to select the best next shot from the input raw footage according to the statistical patterns learned from films. We first evaluate these learned statistics directly by using the FilmGPT autoregressive model for next shot prediction on a standard benchmark of shot sequence ordering, outperforming the previous state of the art. We then evaluate our footage-constrained decoding algorithm on the full film editing task via a user study, and find that our FilmGPT-based editing significantly outperforms previous approaches. Finally, we demonstrate the applicability of FilmGPT to a wide range of applications in video montage, from automatic video segment trimming to human-in-the-loop film editing.

</details>


### [34] [VIABench: A Comprehensive Video Benchmark Collected from Blind Individuals for Visual Impairment Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14660)
*Yunfeng Liu,Yuandong Yang,Jiarui Han,Zhenpeng Huang,Yuqing Tang,Xiangyu Zeng,Gangshan Wu,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIABench是一个专门为评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视障辅助场景中表现而设计的视频基准测试，包含主动提醒、视觉问答和视觉引导交互三个核心任务，实验表明现有MLLMs在视障辅助任务中仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLLMs在通用视觉语言任务上取得了显著成果，但其在真实世界盲人辅助场景中的实用价值尚未被充分探索，缺乏专门针对视障人士需求的评估基准。

Method: 构建VIABench基准，包含主动提醒、视觉问答和视觉引导交互三个任务，采用视障人士自己拍摄或分享的第一人称视频，支持在线（实时）和离线两种评估模式。

Result: 当前MLLMs在视障辅助任务中整体表现不足，尤其在主动提醒任务中，模型在准确预测和实时响应方面存在严重短板。

Conclusion: VIABench有望推动未来研究开发定制化的MLLMs，以真正提升视障人士的导航和交互体验。

Abstract: Visually impaired individuals (VIIs) encounter significant daily challenges due to limited access to visual information. Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive results on general vision and language tasks, their practical utility in real-world blind assistance still remains largely underexplored. To fill this gap, we introduce VIABench, a comprehensive video benchmark specifically designed to evaluate MLLMs in Visually Impaired Assistance scenarios using first-person videos recorded or shared by VIIs themselves. VIABench defines three core tasks, each targeting a distinct requirement in visual assistance. Proactive Reminder: Assesses the model's ability to interpret ongoing video content while proactively anticipating and verbally describing upcoming navigation-critical events; Visual Question Answering (VQA): Evaluates the model's capacity to answer user-posed questions about the environment or objects within the video; Vision-Guided Interaction: Tests context-aware reasoning to accomplish intentional interactions between user and environment. To ensure a robust and fair evaluation, we propose a rigorous benchmarking pipeline that supports both online (real-time) and offline settings. Our experiments demonstrate that current MLLMs still struggle to deliver comprehensive support for VIIs, especially in the Proactive Reminder task, which demands accurate anticipation and real-time responsiveness. We hope VIABench will drive future research toward developing customized MLLMs for real-world assistance, ultimately improving navigation and interaction experiences for visually impaired individuals. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/MCG-NJU/VIABench.

</details>


### [35] [ReBind: Multi-Reference Video Editing via Structured Instructions with Explicit Reference Relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14681)
*Xinyu Liu,Shihao Li,Weihong Lin,Xinlong Chen,Yang Shi,Yujin Han,Yiyang Cai,Yanghao Wang,Ruibin Yuan,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Wenhan Luo,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReBind 框架，通过嵌入引用标记的语义指令作为中间表示，解决多参考图像条件视频编辑中视觉属性与来源之间的精准绑定问题，在指令质量和编辑性能上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的视频编辑模型在处理多参考图像条件时难以准确协调来自多个视觉源的信息，编辑指令缺乏显式引用关系，且大多数多模态大语言模型（MLLM）无法可靠地生成此类引用关系。

Method: 提出 ReBind 系统框架，包含两部分：1) ReBind-Instruct：通过两阶段渐进式方案训练专用 MLLM，学习在语义位置嵌入引用标记以建立视觉属性与其参考源之间的显式绑定；2) ReBind-Edit：实现文本到视频模型的轻量级适配，通过绑定视觉属性到指定来源来协调多参考信息。

Result: 在指令质量上显著优于通用 MLLM，在多参考图像条件视频编辑任务中达到开源方法的最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入带有嵌入引用标记的语义指令作为中间表示，有效消除了多参考图像条件视频编辑中的歧义，建立了视觉属性与其来源之间的精准绑定关系。
