### [135] [CoTu at EXACT 2026: Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning for Transparent Educational QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14735)
*Quoc-Khang Tran,Minh-Thien Nguyen,Phu-An Thai,Xuan-Tung Bui,Truong-Thanh Ma,Nguyen-Khang Pham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文描述了EXACT 2026竞赛中cotu团队开发的神经符号学Program-of-Thought流水线，使用4B参数模型通过编写Z3编码或Python代码来回答大学规章推理和物理问题，在物理任务上获得满分并取得最高技术分（13.44/15），最终排名第三。将答案锚定在符号求解器中可在4B规模下产生正确可验证的推理，剩余难点在于前提选择而非推理本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明教育问答需要既正确又可解释的答案，而使用小模型（最多8B参数）排除了大型专有系统的推理能力。EXACT 2026竞赛提出了这一具体挑战：要求开源权重语言模型自我托管，为每个答案提供自然语言解释，涵盖大学规章逻辑推理和多步物理问题求解两个任务。

Method: 采用神经符号学Program-of-Thought流水线：一个4B骨干模型编写程序而非直接陈述答案——对于规章查询输出Z3编码，通过蕴涵判定来支撑推理；对于物理问题输出数值Python代码。两者共享一个自我修正循环和统一的解释型JSON输出。还包括答案类型路由、基于蒸馏的任务微调以及延迟感知的服务栈（SGLang结合推测解码），以满足每查询60秒的限制。

Result: 该系统在物理任务的自动选拔轮中均获得满分，并在最终轮技术评分中取得所有队伍最高分13.44/15（结合自动答案评估与专家评判的推理深度）。计入同等权重的展示评分后，cotu团队总体排名第三。

Conclusion: 将答案锚定在符号求解器中可以在4B参数规模下产生正确且可验证的推理结论，剩余难点主要在于前提选择而非推理本身。

Abstract: Transparent educational question answering asks for answers that are not only correct but explainable, and doing so with small models rules out the reasoning power of the largest proprietary systems. The EXACT 2026 competition poses this problem concretely: open-weight language models of at most 8B parameters, self-hosted, with a natural-language explanation for every answer. It pairs two tasks: logical reasoning over university regulations, and multi-step physics problem solving. We describe the system that team \cotu{} developed to address both, a neuro-symbolic Program-of-Thought pipeline in which a 4B backbone writes a program rather than stating an answer directly: for regulation queries it emits a Z3 encoding whose entailment verdict grounds the deduction, and for physics it emits numerical Python, both wrapped in a shared self-correction loop and a unified explained-JSON output. Answer-type routing, distillation-based task fine-tuning, and a latency-aware serving stack -- SGLang with speculative decoding -- keep the system within the 60-second per-query limit. The system achieved a \textbf{perfect score} on the physics task in both automated selection rounds and obtained the \textbf{highest final-round technical score} of any team -- $13.44/15$, combining automated answer evaluation with expert-judged reasoning depth -- with the equally weighted presentation score included, \cotu{} placed 3rd overall. Grounding answers in a symbolic solver yields correct, verifiable deductions at the 4B scale, and the residual difficulty lies in premise selection rather than the deduction itself.

</details>


### [136] [Dialogue Summarization with Emotion Dynamics Using Topic- and Participant-Centric Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14769)
*Linyun Xiang,Mark Neerincx,Stephanie Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于层次化Chain-of-Agents的对话摘要框架，通过主题分割和参与者分割结合自动推断的情绪，生成包含语义内容和情绪轨迹的对话摘要，并引入情绪轨迹评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本摘要研究主要关注独白式信息（如新闻文章、报告），未考虑说话者或作者之间的交互。对话是一种丰富的沟通渠道，参与者之间进行来回交流以构建意义，因此需要专门针对对话的摘要方法，同时捕捉语义内容和情感动态。

Method: 提出一个对话摘要框架，基于改进的层次化Chain-of-Agents方法。从两个视角分解对话：(1) 基于所有参与者话语的主题分段，(2) 特定参与者的话语分段。利用这些分段生成摘要并融入自动推断的情绪，然后聚合主题级和参与者级摘要，生成包含语义内容和情绪轨迹的对话摘要。同时引入情绪轨迹指标进行评估。

Result: 在小型语言模型和多模态对话数据集上的实验表明，该框架生成的摘要同时包含语义和情感内容。进一步实验证明了所提方法的有效性，以及利用语言模型进行对话分析的机会。

Conclusion: 提出的对话摘要框架能够有效捕捉对话中的语义内容和情感轨迹，引入的情绪轨迹指标为评估摘要质量提供了新维度。该方法在对话分析中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Existing text summarization research has focused much on monologic information (e.g., newspaper articles, reports) without accounting for the interaction between speakers or authors. In contrast, dialogues are a rich communication channel where multiple participants conduct back and forth exchanges to construct meaning. We propose a dialogue summarization framework that explicitly models both semantic and emotion dynamics using multimodal dialogue inputs, built on an adapted hierarchical Chain-of-Agents approach. We decompose dialogues from two perspectives: (1) topic segments based on the utterances of all participants, and (2) participant-specific utterance segments. These are used to generate corresponding summaries while incorporating automatically inferred emotions. Topic- and participant-level summaries are aggregated into a dialogue summary capturing semantic content and emotion trajectories. To evaluate beyond content accuracy, we introduce emotion trajectory metrics measuring how well summaries preserve emotional flow. Experiments with small language models on multimodal dialogue datasets show that our framework produces summaries with both semantic and emotion content. Further experiments on explicit emotion label availability highlight the efficacy of our proposed methodology and the opportunities in dialogue analysis using language models.

</details>


### [137] [SEED: Self-Evolving On-Policy Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14777)
*Jinyang Wu,Shuo Yang,Zhengxi Lu,Fan Zhang,Yuhao Shen,Lang Feng,Haoran Luo,Zheng Lian,Shuai Zhang,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEED is a self-evolving framework that bridges the supervision gap in outcome-based RL for LLM agents by converting completed trajectories into hindsight skills and distilling them into the policy model via on-policy distillation, improving performance and sample efficiency on text-based and vision-based agentic tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Outcome-based RL for LLM agents provides only sparse trajectory-level rewards, offering limited guidance on intermediate decisions and leaving a supervision gap between episode-level outcomes and token-level policy learning.

Method: SEED first fine-tunes the policy to analyze completed trajectories and generate natural-language skills (reusable workflows, decisive observations, failure-avoidance rules). During RL, the policy both collects trajectories and serves as the analyzer to extract hindsight skills. SEED then re-scores actions under ordinary and skill-augmented contexts, converting skill-induced probability shift into a dense token-level on-policy distillation signal, jointly optimized with outcome-based RL.

Result: Extensive experiments on text-based and vision-based agentic tasks show SEED consistently improves performance and sample efficiency, exhibiting robust generalization to unseen scenarios.

Conclusion: SEED effectively bridges the supervision gap in outcome-based RL for LLM agents by providing dense, self-evolving hindsight supervision that aligns with the current trajectory distribution, leading to better learning and generalization.

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly trained as interactive agents for long-horizon tasks involving multi-turn interaction, tool use, and environment feedback. Outcome-based reinforcement learning (RL) provides a practical optimization paradigm, but its sparse trajectory-level rewards offer limited guidance on intermediate decisions, leaving a supervision gap between episode-level outcomes and token-level policy learning. We propose SEED (SElf-Evolving On-Policy Distillation), a self-evolving framework that converts completed on-policy trajectories into training-time hindsight skills and distills their behavioral effect back into the policy model. SEED first fine-tunes the policy to analyze completed trajectories and generate natural-language skills that capture reusable workflows, decisive observations, or failure-avoidance rules. During RL, the current policy both collects trajectories and serves as the analyzer that extracts hindsight skills from them. Policy updates therefore improve subsequent decision making and skill analysis together, allowing hindsight supervision to evolve with the policy. SEED then re-scores the sampled actions under ordinary and skill-augmented contexts, converting the skill-induced probability shift into a dense token-level on-policy distillation signal. This signal is jointly optimized with outcome-based RL, keeping the auxiliary supervision aligned with the current trajectory distribution. Extensive experiments on text-based and vision-based agentic tasks show that SEED consistently improves performance and sample efficiency, exhibiting robust generalization to unseen scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/jinyangwu/SEED.

</details>


### [138] [Show Me How You Reason and I'll Tell You Who You Are: Reasoning Graphs for Robust LLM Authorship Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14905)
*Zlata Kikteva,Artur Romazanov,Annette Hautli-Janisz,Ramon Ruiz-Dolz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于推理图（reasoning graphs）的图神经网络方法，用于检测LLM生成文本，相比传统基于表层语言特征的方法，在 paraphrase/backtranslation 混淆攻击下性能提升最高27个百分点，在未见模型版本上提升19个百分点，展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成文本检测方法主要依赖表层语言特征（如词汇、句法），容易受到paraphrasing等混淆技术的攻击，亟需一种更鲁棒、能捕捉深层结构信号的检测方法。

Method: 从LLM生成文本中通过argument mining pipeline提取推理图（reasoning graphs），然后使用图神经网络（GNN）对推理结构进行建模和分类，以区分人类撰写文本与LLM生成文本。

Result: 在paraphrasing/backtranslation等混淆攻击下，GNN方法相比传统的Longformer基线最高提升27个百分点；在未见模型版本（模拟持续发布新LLM版本的真实场景）上提升19个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于推理结构的GNN方法比依赖表层特征的检测器更鲁棒、更具泛化能力，能有效应对混淆攻击和模型版本更新带来的挑战。

Abstract: Given the current trend to employ large language models (LLMs) in almost any imaginable context, LLM-generated text detection and authorship attribution have become a pressing issue. Prior work has primarily focused on surface-level linguistic features, an approach shown to be susceptible to paraphrasing and other obfuscation techniques. In this paper, we go beyond the linguistic surface, extracting and analysing reasoning structures in LLM-generated texts with the goal of capturing more complex signals of LLM authorship. We propose a graph neural network approach that leverages reasoning graphs extracted by an argument mining pipeline, demonstrating improved robustness and generalisation over a traditional Longformer baseline. Our approach outperforms the baseline by up to 27 percentage points under the obfuscation attacks such as paraphrasing and backtranslation, and 19 percentage points when evaluated on the texts generated by the unseen model versions, simulating real-world conditions in which new LLM versions are continuously released.

</details>


### [139] [Latent Trajectory Discrimination for AI-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14967)
*Gianluca Bonifazi,Christopher Buratti,Michele Marchetti,Federica Parlapiano,Giulia Quaglieri,Davide Traini,Domenico Ursino,Luca Virgili*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有AI生成文本检测方法将文档视为静态对象、忽略自回归生成动态特性的问题，提出GTCL框架，通过将文档分割为有序局部单元、构建序列级轨迹表示并进行对比学习，利用生成轨迹的几何规律性进行检测，在多个基准上取得一致优于基线方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGTD方法将文档视为静态对象，依赖全局统计或压缩嵌入进行决策，忽略了自回归生成过程中内容在隐空间中逐步演进的内在动态特性。

Method: 提出Geometric Trajectory and Contrastive Learning (GTCL)框架：将文档分割为有序局部单元，对每个单元进行嵌入编码，构建结构化的序列级轨迹表示；然后对轨迹应用对比学习，学习自回归生成相关的几何规律性。

Result: 在三个不同基准和多种方法上的评估表明，GTCL一致优于检测基线，说明显式建模序列动态性能够提供跨模型和跨领域的鲁棒判别信号。

Conclusion: 建模生成轨迹差异能够提升检测性能，为AIGTD领域开辟了一个此前未被充分探索的动态研究方向。

Abstract: Most existing approaches to AI-Generated Text Detection (AIGTD) treat documents as static objects and base their decisions on aggregate statistics or globally compressed embeddings. However, this perspective overlooks the inherently dynamic nature of autoregressive generation, where content evolves progressively through the latent space. In this paper, we reformulate AIGTD as the problem of distinguishing between latent generation trajectories. Instead of relying on static representations, we model how textual representations evolve across the sequence. To this end, we propose Geometric Trajectory and Contrastive Learning (GTCL), a framework that segments the document into ordered local units, encodes each unit in an embedding space, and constructs a structured and sequence-level representation. GTCL then applies contrastive learning to these trajectories to learn geometric regularities associated with the autoregressive generation. Evaluations performed on three different benchmarks and several approaches show that GTCL outperforms detection baselines consistently, which implies that explicitly modeling sequential dynamics provides robust discriminative signals across models and domains. These results suggest that modeling trajectory differences could improve detection and open up a dynamic direction that has been underexplored in previous AIGTD literature.

</details>


### [140] [OmniaBench: Benchmarking General AI Agents Across Diverse Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14989)
*Chengyu Shen,Yujie Fu,Gangtao Xin,Yanheng Hou,Wenlong Fei,Guojie Zhu,Jiawei Li,Hongcheng Gao,Runming He,Zhen Hao Wong,Meiyi Qiang,Hao Liang,Zhao Cao,Hao Jiang,Chong Chen,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OmniaBench is a benchmark for evaluating large language models as general agents across diverse scenarios (ToC, ToB, ToE). It constructs a hierarchical taxonomy of 90 level-1 and 354 level-2 domains, creates executable environments, and synthesizes 1,431 tasks via four routes (DAG, DAG-S, Solver, Program). It introduces a ten-dimensional capability taxonomy and eight atomic difficulty factors for fine-grained analysis. Current frontier models achieve only ~57-58% Pass@1, revealing limitations in planning, constraint maintenance, and adaptive correction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing agent benchmarks are limited in scope, tool ecosystems, or interaction formats, making it difficult to systematically characterize model capabilities across heterogeneous application settings. A broad and diagnostic benchmark is needed to evaluate general agents across diverse scenarios.

Method: The authors derive scenario knowledge from app stores, product documents, industry resources, Web retrieval, and human refinement to form a hierarchical taxonomy spanning ToC, ToB, ToE with 90 level-1 and 354 level-2 domains. They construct executable environments and synthesize single-turn and multi-turn tasks via four complementary routes: DAG, DAG-S, Solver, and Program. They also introduce a ten-dimensional capability taxonomy and eight compositional atomic difficulty factors.

Result: The benchmark contains 1,431 tasks (including a challenging subset of 644 tasks). Current frontier models like Claude-Sonnet-5 and GPT-5.6-Sol achieve Overall Pass@1 scores of only 58.54 and 57.14 respectively. Analysis reveals clear differences across domains and capabilities, with persistent limitations in planning, constraint maintenance, and adaptive correction.

Conclusion: OmniaBench provides a broad and diagnostic benchmark for characterizing the capability boundaries of general agents, highlighting significant challenges for current models and areas needing improvement.

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly evolving from text generators into general agents capable of understanding user requests, invoking external tools, and completing complex tasks through interaction. However, existing agent benchmarks often focus on limited scenarios, tool ecosystems, or interaction formats, making it difficult to systematically characterize model capabilities across heterogeneous application settings. We introduce OmniaBench, a benchmark for evaluating general agents across diverse scenarios with explicit state spaces. We derive application-oriented scenario knowledge from app stores, product documents, industry resources, Web retrieval, and human refinement, forming a hierarchical taxonomy that spans ToC, ToB and ToE with 90 level-1 and 354 level-2 domains. Based on this taxonomy, we construct executable environments and synthesize single-turn and multi-turn tasks through four complementary routes: DAG, DAG-S, Solver, and Program. OmniaBench further introduces a ten-dimensional capability taxonomy and eight compositional atomic difficulty factors to support fine-grained evaluation and analysis. The resulting dataset contains 1,431 tasks, together with a challenging subset of 644 tasks designed to reduce evaluation cost and mitigate potential contamination of the full set after public release. The bench presents substantial challenges to current frontier models, with even Claude-Sonnet-5 and GPT-5.6-Sol achieving Overall Pass@1 scores of only 58.54 and 57.14, respectively. Further analyses reveal clear differences across domains and capabilities, as well as persistent limitations in planning, constraint maintenance, and adaptive correction. OmniaBench provides a broad and diagnostic benchmark for characterizing the capability boundaries of general agents.

</details>


### [141] [Rubrics on Trial: Evolving Rubrics from a Single Query via Synthetic Pairwise Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15092)
*Haocheng Yang,Licheng Pan,Xiaoxi Li,Zhichao Chen,Zhiheng Zhang,Yuan Lu,Haoxuan Li,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rubrics on Trial 框架，无需外部标注或模型训练，仅通过查询（query）自动生成并验证评分标准（rubrics），在五个偏好基准测试中取得最佳平均准确率，并在七个评估集上领先六个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的查询特定评分标准（rubrics）难以构建。现有方法依赖人工编写的rubrics、偏好数据或采样响应，而直接查询到rubric生成缺乏对rubric有用性的显式检验，可能导致rubric无法区分答案质量、奖励可选风格或惩罚有效的替代策略。

Method: 提出 Rubrics on Trial 框架，一种仅基于查询的框架，从空集开始演化rubric集合，无需外部标注或模型训练。它仅从合成的rubric条件响应对中获取监督信号，并在添加每个候选rubric之前进行验证，筛选掉非区分性、过度特定和仅基于风格的候选rubric。

Result: 在五个偏好基准测试套件上的实验表明，Rubrics on Trial 达到了最佳平均准确率，并在七个评估集中领先六个。

Conclusion: Rubrics on Trial 框架能够自动生成有用且可靠的rubrics，无需外部资源，有效提升LLM评估质量。

Abstract: Rubrics provide structured, fine-grained signals for training and evaluating large language models (LLMs). Yet reliable query-specific rubrics are difficult to construct. Existing approaches often derive supervision from human-written rubrics, preference data, or sampled responses. Direct query-to-rubric generation avoids these resources, but provides no explicit check that a plausible rubric is useful. Such a rubric may fail to distinguish answer quality, reward an optional style, or penalize a valid alternative strategy. We introduce Rubrics on Trial, a query-only framework that evolves a rubric set from an empty set without external annotations or model training. It derives supervision solely from synthetic rubric-conditioned response pairs and validates each proposed rubric before adding it, screening out non-discriminative, over-specific, and style-only candidate rubrics. Experiments across five preference benchmark suites demonstrate the effectiveness of Rubrics on Trial, which achieves the best average accuracy and leads on six of seven evaluation sets.

</details>


### [142] [Digital Pantheon: Simulating and Auditing Coalition Formation with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15095)
*Dylan Van Mulders,Matthias Bogaert,Dirk Van den Poel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合监督微调、直接偏好优化和检索增强生成的多智能体框架，用于模拟政治联盟谈判。在2019年佛兰德选举中，该框架通过党派智能体在形式化调解下的谈判，生成可解释的联盟协议，并通过多层级信息溯源拓扑和联盟影响力分数追溯条款来源。三次独立模拟产生稳定胜者（N-VA领先），且基于宣言的溯源能可靠预测真实联盟协议内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽为计算政治学开辟新途径，但RLHF带来的中立性和助益性偏见使其难以维持坚定的党派行为。现有方法无法同时实现事实基础与意识形态对齐，需要一种能模拟政治联盟谈判中复杂博弈的透明、可扩展框架。

Method: 采用多智能体框架，结合监督微调、直接偏好优化和检索增强生成：DPO注入攻击性党派特定人格，每个党派配置独立RAG管道以约束其官方宣言。在2019年佛兰德选举数据集上运行枢纽-辐式谈判（由形式化调解）。引入多层级信息溯源拓扑（MILT）将协议条款追溯至宣言来源并分类为五种溯源状态，以及联盟影响力分数（CIS）聚合贡献以识别主导党派。

Result: 三次独立模拟产生稳定胜者和排名（N-VA领先CD&V和Open Vld）。基于宣言的溯源可靠预测真实联盟协议实现内容，而幻觉内容则不能。框架提供透明的、可扩展的前期测试床，用于探索党派兼容性和形式化调解的妥协。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将事实基础与意识形态对齐相结合，生成可解释的联盟谈判模拟，并能预测真实世界协议。为计算政治学提供了新的工具，可用于在真实谈判前评估联盟可能性及党派影响力。

Abstract: The formation of political coalitions is a complex negotiation driven by both concrete policy objectives and deep-seated ideological convictions. While Large Language Models (LLMs) open new avenues for computational political science, the neutrality and helpfulness biases instilled by Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) prevent them from sustaining steadfast partisan behaviour. We present a multi-agent framework that reconciles factual grounding with ideological alignment by combining Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG): DPO instils aggressive party-specific personas, while a per-party RAG pipeline keeps each agent bounded to its official manifesto. We operationalize the framework on the 2019 Flemish election, deploying the partisan agents in a hub-and-spoke negotiation arbitrated by a formateur. To make the emergent negotiation interpretable, we introduce a Multi-Layered Information Lineage Topology (MILT) that traces every clause in the final agreement back to its manifesto origin and classifies it into five provenance states, a Coalition Influence Score (CIS) that aggregates these traceable contributions to identify which party shaped the agreement, and a real-world grounding pass that benchmarks each simulated provision against the historically adopted coalition agreement. Across three independent simulations the framework yields a stable winner and ranking (N-VA ahead of CD\&V and Open Vld), and manifesto-anchored lineage reliably predicts real-world materialization whereas hallucinated content does not. The result is a transparent, scalable testbed for the ex-ante exploration of party compatibility and formateur-mediated compromise.

</details>


### [143] [Grokipedia vs Wikipedia: An LLM-Based Audit of Political Neutrality along Ideologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15146)
*Filippos Vlahos,Guillaume Bied,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Grokipedia（由LLM Grok编写的百科全书）被所有LLM评判者（包括Grok自身）评为不如维基百科中立。两者均对政治家有正面偏向，但针对不同意识形态群体：Grokipedia偏向经济右翼、惩罚社会自由派，维基百科则偏向社会自由派。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维基百科被指控存在"左翼"和"自由主义"偏见，Grokipedia旨在提供无偏见的替代方案。本研究检验LLM编写的百科全书是否真的更中立，还是只是嵌入了不同的意识形态。

Method: 对Grokipedia和维基百科的1,394篇描述政府成员的文章进行配对，使用四个LLM评判者（Grok、Claude、Mistral、DeepSeek）沿九位专家编码的意识形态维度评估中立性，并分析LLM自身可能的偏见模式。

Result: 所有LLM评判者（包括Grok）均认为Grokipedia不如维基百科中立。两部百科全书总体上对政治家有正面偏向，但针对不同群体：Grokipedia偏向经济右翼政治家、惩罚社会自由派；维基百科偏向社会自由派。

Conclusion: LLM编写的百科全书并未提供更中立的内容，而是反映了不同的意识形态偏好。Grokipedia在经济和社会维度上表现出与维基百科相反的偏见模式。

Abstract: Online encyclopedias shape political opinion and, through it, democratic discourse. In late 2025, Grokipedia was released, an encyclopedia written entirely by the LLM Grok. One motivation behind the project was to provide an unbiased alternative to Wikipedia, which has faced accusations of "left-wing" and "liberal" bias. But does an encyclopedia written by an LLM deliver greater neutrality, or does it simply embed a different ideology? We conduct a large-scale political bias study on Grokipedia and Wikipedia, analysing 1,394 article pairs describing members of government for neutrality along nine expert-coded ideology dimensions employing four LLM judges, Grok, Claude, Mistral, and DeepSeek. As the LLMs could themselves be biased, we also investigate patterns in their judgments. We find all LLM-judges, including Grok, to rate Grokipedia less neutral than Wikipedia. Both encyclopedias are rated as portraying politicians favourably overall, but towards different ideological groups. Grokipedia particularly favours economically right-wing politicians and penalises socially liberal ones, while Wikipedia is rated as favourably biased towards the latter.

</details>


### [144] [Linear representations of grammaticality in neural language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15175)
*Jane Li,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过质量均值探测法，发现神经语言模型在表征空间中系统性地区分了语法正确与错误的句子，且这种区分不能被词汇频率、合理性等表层因素完全解释，并能跨语法现象和部分跨语言泛化，为语言模型具备语法知识提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要依赖概率比较来评估语言模型对语法性的区分能力，但该方法备受批评，因为模型分配的概率受词汇频率、合理性、世界知识等多种因素影响，语法性与似然性本质上纠缠不清。因此，需要超越概率比较，探索语法性是否在模型内部表征中被编码。

Method: 采用质量均值探测法，在多种预训练神经语言模型的句子表征空间中，检验语法正确与语法错误的句子是否系统性地分离；同时控制与语法性相关的替代句子属性（如词汇频率、合理性等），并评估这种表征跨语法现象和跨语言的泛化能力。

Result: 语法性在多种预训练语言模型的句子表征中被稳健编码，表征空间在语法性维度上呈现清晰分离，且不能被其他句子层面的因素完全解释；这种编码能泛化到多种语法现象，并在一定程度上跨语言泛化。

Conclusion: 语法性是当代神经语言模型中一个连贯的表征维度，这为语言模型是否具备语法知识的争论提供了新证据，并提出了一种不依赖字符串概率的语法能力评估框架。

Abstract: Whether neural language models (NLMs) possess the ability to distinguish strings on the basis of their grammaticality remains a debated topic in the computational linguistics literature. Existing evidence has largely relied on probability-based measures, testing whether models assign higher probabilities to grammatical than ungrammatical strings. However, probability comparisons have been criticized as a measure for grammatical knowledge based on the assumption that grammaticality is inherently entangled with likelihood. Model-assigned probability is a function of many related sentence properties, such as lexical frequency, plausibility, and world knowledge. In this work, we move beyond probability-based evaluations and investigate whether grammaticality is encoded in the internal representations of NLMs. Using mass-mean probing, we test whether grammatical and ungrammatical sentences are systematically separated in representational space. We further examine the extent to which these representations are independent of sentence properties that are correlated with grammaticality, as well as their generalization across grammatical phenomena and languages. Our results provide evidence that grammaticality is robustly encoded in sentence representations of a wide range of pretrained NLMs, yielding clear representational separation on the dimension of grammaticality that cannot be fully explained by alternative sentence-level factors. Moreover, this encoding generalizes across a broad range of grammatical phenomena and to some degree, across languages, suggesting that grammaticality constitutes a coherent representational dimension in contemporary NLMs. These findings contribute new evidence to debates about the nature of syntactic knowledge in language models and offer a complementary framework for evaluating grammatical competence that is not dependent on string probabilities alone.

</details>


### [145] [T^2MLR: Transformer with Temporal Middle-Layer Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15178)
*Ziyang Cai,Xingyu Zhu,Yihe Dong,Yinghui He,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为T2MLR的Transformer变体，通过在中间层引入时序递归机制（将前一个token的中间层表示缓存并注入当前token的浅层），使抽象中间计算能在解码步骤间持续存在，从而提升推理能力。实验表明，仅对局部中间层（20%的网络）施加递归即可超越全层递归，且可直接改造已有预训练模型，无需从头训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的自回归解码限制了推理能力：每一步都将丰富的隐藏层计算反复压缩到token空间，导致中间推理状态难以跨时间步持续存在，阻碍了复杂多步推理的有效进行。

Method: 提出T2MLR架构，核心是在Transformer的中间层（而非所有层）引入跨时间步的递归连接：将前一个token位置某中间层的隐藏表示缓存，并直接注入当前token对应位置的更浅层，使得抽象中间计算信息能够跨越解码步骤传递，且推理开销极小。

Result: 在自然语言预训练和多跳推理微调中，T2MLR持续优于同等数据量和参数量的Transformer基线。仅对局部中间层（20%网络）施加递归的效果常常优于全层递归。此外，可直接将递归路径改造进已有的1.7B预训练Transformer，经简短微调即可显著提升数学推理性能。

Conclusion: 有效的潜在推理不需要像先前工作那样对所有层进行循环；相反，针对中间层的递归更高效且更强，能以较低门槛实际部署。

Abstract: Transformer reasoning is limited by autoregressive decoding, which repeat edly compresses rich hidden computation through token space and makes it difficult for intermediate reasoning states to persist across time. We in troduce Transformers with Temporal Middle-Layer Recurrence (T2MLR), a transformers-based latent reasoning architecture that fuses a cached middle layer representation from the previous token directly into an earlier layer of the current token position, enabling abstract intermediate computation to persist across decoding steps with little inference overhead. Across natural-language pretraining and multi-hop reasoning finetuning, T2MLR consistently outperforms data- and parameter-matched Transformer base lines. Moreover, applying recurrence to only a localized middle-layer block (as little as 20% of the network) often outperforms full-layer recurrence. Im portantly, T2MLR does not require pretraining from scratch: retrofitting the recurrent pathway into an existing pretrained 1.7B Transformer and briefly finetuning substantially improves math reasoning, lowering the barrier to practical adoption. These results suggest that effective latent reasoning in Transformers does not require looping over all layers as in previous works, but can instead emerge more strongly from targeted middle-layer recurrence.

</details>


### [146] [Mask-Aware Policy Gradients for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15200)
*Haran Raajesh,Kulin Shah,Adam Klivans,Philipp Krähenbühl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的强化学习方法，将生成过程形式化为两阶段动作MDP，通过联合优化token预测和掩码决策，在数学推理和代码生成基准上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法难以直接应用于MDLM，因为其对数似然估计难以处理；已有近似方法只考虑token预测而忽略掩码顺序，导致生成策略不完整。

Method: 将MDLM生成过程建模为两阶段动作MDP，每个步骤包含两种决策：在掩码位置放置什么token以及哪些位置需要重新掩码。策略梯度自然分解为token项和掩码项，联合优化两者。

Result: 在数学推理基准GSM8K上达到87.1%，在代码生成基准MBPP上达到53.4%，均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模掩码决策并联合优化token和掩码策略，可以显著提升MDLM的推理能力，表明强化学习在扩散语言模型中具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven effective for improving reasoning in large language models, but extending it to Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) remains challenging due to the intractability of the log-likelihood estimation. Existing approaches approximate this log-likelihood by modeling only the token predictions, ignoring the order in which positions are unmasked during generation. We observe that MDLM generation involves two decisions at each step: what tokens to place at each masked position and which positions to remask. We formalize this as a two-stage action MDP, showing that the policy gradient naturally decomposes into a token term and a masking term. Combining optimization of both terms leads to state-of-the-art outcomes on mathematical reasoning and coding benchmarks, with scores of 87.1% on GSM8K and 53.4% on MBPP.

</details>


### [147] [Expanding the Lexicon of Ge'ez Based African Languages: A Comparative Study of Amharic and Tigrinya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15209)
*Hailay Kidu Teklehaymanot,Debela Desalegn Yadeta,Wolfgang Nejdl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 VEXMLM，通过扩展 XLM-R 词表加入 30,000 个 Ge'ez 子词，并在 Amharic/Tigrinya 上继续预训练+微调，显著提升低资源非洲语言的任务性能（QA、NER、SA），同时改善词表覆盖率和 OOV 问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言 PLM（如 XLM-R）在低资源、非拉丁文字语言上表现差，根源在于基于拉丁语的 tokenizer 训练导致高 OOV 率和过度子词碎片化。针对 Ge'ez 脚本（Amharic 和 Tigrinya）进行词表扩展以缓解该问题。

Method: 使用针对 Amharic/Tigrinya 的 SentencePiece tokenizer 训练，将生成的 30,000 Ge'ez 子词加入 XLM-R 词表，新子词嵌入初始化为原 tokenizer 下对应片段嵌入的平均值。两阶段训练：第一阶段在扩展词表上对目标语料继续 MLM 预训练；第二阶段在 QA、NER、SA 任务上监督微调。评估覆盖 19 种非洲语言（含 Amharic/Tigrinya 及 17 种低资源语言）。

Result: Amharic/Tigrinya QA：VEXMLM 达 87.0 EM/90.0 F1，远超 XLM-R (66.0/78.0) 和 Glot500 (74.0/78.0)；SA 准确率 80.0% vs XLM-R 77.0%、Glot500 46.0%；NER 中 OOV 实体准确率从 81.4% 提升至 94.3%（平均 11 种语言）。词表覆盖率和子词碎片的固有指标也显著改善。

Conclusion: 词表扩展与嵌入初始化策略针对 Ge'ez 脚本有效；两阶段训练使 Amharic/Tigrinya 上的增益泛化到 17 种相关低资源语言；综合评估涵盖内在 tokenization 指标与外在任务性能，证实该方法能显著提升低资源非拉丁文字语言的多语言 PLM 表现。

Abstract: Multilingual pre-trained language models (PLMs) exhibit degraded performance on low-resource, non-Latin-script languages, driven by high out-of-vocabulary (OOV) rates and excessive subword fragmentation that result from Latin-script-centric tokenizer training. We introduce VEXMLM, a vocabulary-extended variant of XLM-R targeting the two highest-resource Ge'ez-script languages, Amharic and Tigrinya, and further evaluated on 17 additional low-resource African languages (19 total). We train a language-specific SentencePiece tokenizer on curated Amharic and Tigrinya monolingual corpora, extend XLM-R's vocabulary with 30,000 Ge'ez-script subwords derived from this tokenizer, and initialize their embeddings by averaging the embeddings of their constituent subwords under XLM-R's original tokenizer. VEXMLM is trained in two stages: (1) continued masked language modeling over the extended vocabulary on the curated corpora, and (2) supervised fine-tuning on question answering (QA), named entity recognition (NER), and sentiment analysis (SA). On Amharic/Tigrinya QA, VEXMLM achieves 87.0 EM /90.0 F1, versus 66.0 EM/78.0 F1 for XLM-R and 74.0 EM/ 78.0 F1 for Glot500. On SA, VEXMLM reaches 80.0\% accuracy versus 77.0\% (XLM-R) and 46.0\% (Glot500). On NER, VEXMLM raises OOV-token entity accuracy from 81.4\% to 94.3\%, averaged over 11 of the 19 evaluated languages for which OOV analysis was possible. Our contributions are: (i) a vocabulary-extension and embedding-initialization procedure tailored to Ge'ez script; (ii) a two-stage training strategy under which vocabulary and continued-pretraining gains on Amharic/Tigrinya transfer to 17 typologically related, unaugmented African languages; and (iii) an evaluation spanning both intrinsic tokenization metrics (vocabulary coverage, fertility, OOV rate) and extrinsic task performance across all 19 languages.

</details>


### [148] [In-Place Tokenizer Expansion for Pre-trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15232)
*Jimmy T. H. Smith,Tarek Dakhran,Alberto Cabrera,Simon S. Lee,Paul Pak,Aditya Tadimeti,Tim Seyde,Maxime Labonne,Alexander Amini,Mathias Lechner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种“分词器扩展”（tokenizer expansion）方法，在不重新训练整个模型的前提下，为预训练模型升级分词器词汇表，使得新语言（如印地语、越南语、泰语）的编码更高效，从而降低延迟、计算和能耗。在LFM2-8B-A1B混合专家模型上验证，将词汇表扩展至128K，新语言每字符解码速度提升2.2-3.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始分词器在预训练开始时根据语料分布固定词汇表，当后期需要支持新语言时，这些语言会被分割成更多子词，导致推理延迟、计算量和能耗增加。云端模型词汇表大影响小，但紧凑的端侧模型词汇表占参数比例大，只能使用小词汇表，导致语言碎片化严重。因此需要一种能在线升级分词器的方法。

Method: 1. 在现有分词器的BPE合并基础上，继续在多语言语料上进行合并，使大部分源token保持不变，每个新token可精确分解为源子词。2. 复制已有的embedding行，新token的embedding初始化为其源子词embedding的均值。3. 两阶段适应：先只训练embedding，再对整个模型进行持续预训练，恢复原始质量。

Result: 在LFM2-8B-A1B（8B参数MoE模型）上应用，将分词器扩展至128K。印地语和越南语分别减少约2.4倍和2.6倍的token数，泰语减少4.0倍。结合大词汇表的每token成本，在参考设备上这些语言每字符解码速度提升2.2-3.7倍。

Conclusion: 分词器扩展是一种有效的原位升级方法，能在不牺牲已有语言质量的前提下，显著提升新语言的编码效率和解码速度。该方法适用于模型生产者控制设计的场景，已发布模型权重和扩展后的分词器，并报告了负面发现以指导后续研究。

Abstract: A tokenizer fixed at the start of pre-training allocates vocabulary in proportion to the pre-training corpus, reflecting the deployment priorities at that time. When those priorities shift, languages added later are split into many more tokens per word, which can raise latency, compute, and energy consumption for users of those languages. Cloud models can afford a broad vocabulary because the embedding and LM-head matrices are a small fraction of their parameters. On a compact model those matrices are a material share of per-token decode bandwidth, so on-device models ship small vocabularies and accept fragmentation outside a fixed language set. We present tokenizer expansion, an in-place recipe for upgrading a pre-trained model's tokenizer when the model producer controls its design. We continue the existing tokenizer's BPE merges on a multilingual corpus, so most source tokens carry over unchanged as single tokens and every new token has an exact decomposition into source tokens. We copy the carried-over embedding rows unchanged and initialize new rows as the mean of their source sub-token embeddings. A two-stage adaptation, embedding-only training then full-model continued pre-training, recovers source-checkpoint quality. We apply the recipe to a continued pre-trained checkpoint of LFM2-8B-A1B, an 8B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts model, to help produce LFM2.5-8B-A1B with a 128K tokenizer. The expanded tokenizer encodes Hindi and Vietnamese in roughly $2.4\times$ and $2.6\times$ fewer tokens than the source (up to $4.0\times$ on Thai). Combining these reductions with the measured per-token cost of the larger vocabulary, we estimate a $2.2$-$3.7\times$ per-character decode speedup for these languages across our reference devices. We release the model weights and the expanded tokenizer, and report the negative findings that shaped the recipe.

</details>


### [149] [Language Identification via Compositional Data Analysis: A Linear-Time Classifier Based on Log-Ratio Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15238)
*Paul-Andrei Pogăcean,Sanda-Maria Avram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于成分数据分析的语言识别方法，将字符和双字母频率分布视为成分向量，通过中心化对数比变换映射到欧几里得空间，结合拉普拉斯平滑处理稀疏性，在六种语言上取得了鲁棒的准确率，为语言识别提供了一种确定性、计算高效且可解释的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络方法在语言识别中计算资源需求高，而传统的基于频率的n-gram方法虽然线性时间高效，但使用的距离度量不适用于成分数据（即频数分布这类总和为常数的数据）。因此需要一种既能保持线性效率又能恰当处理成分数据特性的方法。

Method: 将字符和双字母频率分布建模为约束在单纯形上的成分向量，通过中心化对数比变换将其双射映射到(D-1)维零和子空间，使得欧几里得距离等价于Aitchison距离。构建了一个结合CLR变换后的单字母和双字母特征、并采用拉普拉斯平滑处理稀疏性的流水线。

Result: 在六种语言上的实验表明，所提方法在不同文本长度下均能实现鲁棒的准确率，尤其是在较长文本序列上表现优异。

Conclusion: 成分表示方法为语言识别提供了一种确定性、计算高效的替代方案，特别适用于需要可解释性和低资源消耗的场景。

Abstract: Language identification is commonly addressed using either neural architectures or statistical n-gram models. Neural approaches typically require substantial computational resources, whereas classical frequency-based methods offer efficient linear-time performance, but rely on distance metrics that are not always appropriate for compositional data.
  This work models character and bigram frequency distributions as compositional vectors constrained to the simplex and mapped via the centered log-ratio (CLR) transformation bijectively onto the $(D-1)$-dimensional zero-sum subspace of $\mathbb{R}^D$, where Euclidean distances correspond to Aitchison distances. A pipeline is proposed, combining CLR-transformed unigram and bigram features with Laplace smoothing to address sparsity. The method is evaluated on six languages.
  Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves robust accuracy across different text lengths, with strong performance for longer sequences. These findings indicate that compositional representations provide a deterministic and computationally efficient alternative for language identification, particularly in settings where interpretability and low resource consumption are essential.

</details>


### [150] [TikStance: A Multimodal and Hierarchical Dataset for Multi-target Stance Analysis in TikTok Political Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15240)
*Yazhi Zhang,Fuqiang Niu,Bowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TikStance is a multimodal, context-aware dataset from TikTok containing 161 videos and 13,876 comments on three major 2024 U.S. election candidates (Trump, Biden, Harris), with hierarchical conversation structures and reliable three-class stance annotations (Favor, Against, None) for both video-to-target and comment-to-target stance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Political discourse has shifted to short-video platforms, but existing datasets lack joint preservation of audiovisual information and hierarchical conversations, limiting computational analysis of stance in such contexts.

Method: The dataset comprises 161 TikTok videos and 13,876 comments collected from September 2023 to January 2025, covering Trump, Biden, and Harris. Each item links a host video with metadata to a parent-linked comment tree. Three annotators independently labeled each item using a three-class scheme (Favor, Against, None); disagreements were re-annotated to ensure reliability.

Result: Final Krippendorff's α reached 0.743, 0.723, and 0.722 for Trump, Biden, and Harris subsets, respectively. Nested replies account for 23.3% of all comments, and target-dependent differences in stance distributions and conversational depth were observed.

Conclusion: By combining multi-target coverage, hierarchical conversations, and reliable multi-level annotations, TikStance enables research in multimodal stance detection, political communication, computational social science, and context-aware NLP.

Abstract: Political discourse has increasingly moved to short-video platforms, yet computational analysis of such content remains constrained by the scarcity of datasets that jointly preserve audiovisual information and hierarchical conversations. Here we present TikStance, a multimodal and context-aware dataset comprising 161 videos and 13,876 comments from TikTok, designed for stance detection in political discussions. The dataset covers three major political figures in the 2024 U.S. election cycle--Donald Trump, Joe Biden, and Kamala Harris--with content collected between September 2023 and January 2025. Each discussion unit links a host video and its metadata to a parent-linked comment tree, enabling stance analysis within both audiovisual and conversational context. Each item was independently labeled by three annotators using a three-class scheme (Favor, Against, None) for video-to-target and comment-to-target stance; items with disagreement were re-annotated, and the final Krippendorff's \(α\) reached 0.743, 0.723, and 0.722 for the Trump, Biden, and Harris subsets, respectively. Descriptive analysis further reveals target-dependent differences in stance distributions and conversational depth, with nested replies accounting for 23.3\% of all comments. By combining multi-target coverage, hierarchical conversations, and reliable multi-level human annotations, TikStance supports research in multimodal stance detection, political communication, computational social science, and context-aware natural language processing.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond the Leaderboard: Design Lessons for Trustworthy Multimodal VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15241)
*Sushant Gautam,Vajira Thambawita,Michael A. Riegler,Pål Halvorsen,Steven A. Hicks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以MediaEval Medico 2025胃肠道内窥镜回顾性研究为案例，分析了九个多模态AI系统在问答和解释质量上的设计选择。研究发现参数高效微调能提升性能，但答案准确性并不保证临床推理的忠实性与完整性；而结构化推理和显式接地方法在不同问题类型上表现更可靠。研究呼吁超越词汇重叠的评估、标准化证据链接解释、防泄漏数据治理以及轻量级鲁棒性和校准检查，以支持可信的多模态医疗AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗多模态AI需要融合视觉和文本证据，同时保持可靠性和可解释性。当前缺乏对系统设计选择如何影响问答性能和解释质量的系统性分析，特别是在需要忠实且完整的临床推理的医疗场景中。

Method: 以MediaEval Medico 2025胃肠道内窥镜任务为回顾性案例，分析九个已文档化的系统在问答和解释质量方面的设计选择，比较参数高效微调、结构化推理和显式接地等方法的效果。

Result: 参数高效适配的预训练骨干能带来强挑战性能，但答案层面的提升并未一致转化为忠实且完整的临床推理；采用结构化推理和显式接地的方法在不同问题类型上表现更可靠，但证据是相关性的而非基于消融实验。

Conclusion: 需要超越词汇重叠的评估指标、标准化的证据链接解释、防泄漏数据治理以及轻量级鲁棒性和校准检查，以支持基于数据融合、可解释性和弹性评估的可信多模态医疗AI。

Abstract: Healthcare multimodal AI must combine visual and textual evidence while remaining reliable and interpretable. Using MediaEval Medico 2025 as a retrospective GI endoscopy case study, we analyze design choices across nine documented systems for question answering and explanation quality. Parameter-efficient adaptation of pretrained backbones provides strong challenge performance, but answer-level gains do not consistently translate into faithful and complete clinical reasoning. Methods enforcing structured reasoning and explicit grounding show more reliable behavior across heterogeneous question types, although the evidence is correlational rather than ablation-based. These results motivate evaluation beyond lexical overlap, standardized evidence-linked explanations, leakage-aware data governance, and lightweight robustness and calibration checks. The findings support trustworthy multimodal healthcare AI based on data fusion, explainability, and resilient evaluation.

</details>


### [152] [SciDiagramEdit: Learning to Edit Scientific Diagrams from Paper Revisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15272)
*Yasheng Sun,Zezi Zeng,Yifan Yang,Chong Luo,Wenyi Wang,Ziwei Liu,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 SciDiagramEdit，一个基于自然语言指令编辑科学论文图表的基准与技能进化框架。通过挖掘 arXiv 版本历史中的前后对比图对，并采用智能体学习与技能进化机制，使得模型能够从自然的论文修订中学习并提升编辑准确性，验证了论文修订作为训练信号的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编辑研究论文中的图表是日常研究工作中常规但耗时的任务，而由于科学图表是包含示意图、曲线图、照片、标题和箭头等多种视觉元素的密集信息图，其视觉语法严谨且服务于特定论点，因此通过自然语言指令自动化这一编辑流程极具挑战性。

Method: 构建 SciDiagramEdit 基准，从 arXiv 版本历史中挖掘前后对比的图表对，并关联作者自身的修订意图。采用智能体学习（agentic learning）与技能进化（skill evolution）框架：一个智能体提议者（agentic proposer）通过多轮执行轨迹（execution traces）持续优化智能体的技能规范（skill specification），从而逐步提升编辑准确性。

Result: 在保留的验证集上，经过技能进化后，编辑准确性逐步提升，证明自然论文修订可以作为指令驱动图表编辑的有效训练信号。

Conclusion: 自然论文修订是指导图表编辑的有效训练信号，为自动化图表编辑提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Editing the figures in a research paper is a routine and time-consuming part of everyday research practice: authors relabel components, rearrange panels, and restyle visuals as they revise their manuscripts. Automating this editing workflow under a natural-language instruction, however, is challenging, because a scientific figure is a dense infographic in which heterogeneous visual elements such as schematics, plots, photos, captions, and arrows are composed under a tight visual grammar to advance a specific argument. To address this, we present SciDiagramEdit, a benchmark and skill-evolution framework that learns from natural paper revisions and operates on the figure's editable vector source, where users can inspect and co-edit individual primitives alongside the agent. Our benchmark mines before/after figure pairs from arXiv version histories, each grounded in the authors' own revision intent. To accommodate the diversity of editing instructions, we adopt agentic learning via skill evolution: an agentic proposer continually refines the agent's skill specification from execution traces over multiple epochs. The resulting skill progressively lifts edit accuracy on a held-out validation set, providing evidence that natural paper revisions are an effective training signal for instruction-driven figure editing.

</details>


### [153] [Partition, Prompt, Aggregate: Statistical Self-Consistency in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15277)
*Patrik Wolf,Thomas Kleine Buening,Andreas Krause,Celestine Mendler-Dünner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过二叉树递归划分总体，检验LLM在上下文学习中的概率自一致性（全概率公式），发现模型普遍违反该原则，并揭示一种“宏观谬误”：细粒度子群体估计比直接总体估计更符合人类参考数据。这促使将统计自一致性作为无需参考的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习常被视为条件推断，若此解释成立，LLM的估计应满足概率恒等式如全概率公式。作者旨在验证LLM估计是否遵循这一自一致性原则，从而判断模型的推断行为是否具有概率一致性。

Method: 采用二叉树作为评估框架，递归地将总体划分为粒度递增的子群体。对LLM输入口语化的子群体描述（上下文中的提示），聚合其估计值还原为总体估计，并比较不同划分粒度下的估计结果，跨问题领域和前沿模型进行系统测试。

Result: 发现广泛的自一致性违反；进一步揭示“宏观谬误”：从更细粒度子群体聚合的估计比直接总体估计更准确（与人类参考数据对齐更好），该现象在不同树结构和任务中稳定，并可通过隐式提示部分缓解。

Conclusion: 模型具备子群体相关知识但无法将其可靠地传递到聚合估计中，这一定位使得统计自一致性成为一项未饱和的、无需参考的LLM评估标准，可用于衡量模型的条件推断能力。

Abstract: In-context learning is commonly interpreted as a form of conditional inference, in which the prompt specifies a context and the model's output is treated as an estimate of the corresponding conditional distribution. If this interpretation holds, then LLM estimates should satisfy basic probabilistic identities. In particular, the law of total probability asserts that prior-weighted conditional distributions aggregate into population-level marginals over any valid partition of the population. In this work, we investigate to what extent LLM estimates adhere to this self-consistency principle. We use binary trees as an evaluation scaffold to recursively partition a population into increasingly fine-grained subpopulations. We then prompt LLMs with verbalized subpopulation descriptions in context, aggregate the resulting estimates back into population-level estimates, and compare them across partitions of varying granularity. Applying this protocol across problem domains and state-of-the-art frontier models, we show widespread violations of basic consistency properties. An in-depth study of persona prompting reveals a pattern we call the macro fallacy: estimates reconstructed from more fine-grained subpopulation responses are often better aligned with human reference data than direct population-level estimates. This effect persists across variations in tree structure and estimation task, and can be partially recovered through implicit prompting. Together, these findings suggest that models possess relevant subpopulation knowledge but do not reliably propagate it into aggregate estimates. This gap establishes statistical self-consistency as an unsaturated, reference-free criterion for evaluating LLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Position: Explainability Research Must Prioritize Foundations over Ad-hoc Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14123)
*Michal Moshkovitz,Suraj Srinivas,Lesia Semenova,Nave Frost,Cyrus Rashtchian,Valentyn Boreiko,Shichang Zhang,Himabindu Lakkaraju,Cynthia Rudin,Jennifer Wortman Vaughan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前XAI技术虽多，但很少真正影响实际工作流程，主要原因是缺乏将解释整合到端到端、人在环路系统中的方法论。文章通过分析顶会论文和从业者调查，呼吁学界从开发临时性XAI方法转向解决基础性结构挑战，并提出了一个面向人类中心、行动导向范式的实用清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管XAI技术（如特征归因、稀疏自编码器）层出不穷，但这些解释在实际工作流中很少被真正使用，往往是生成后被丢弃。这种差距反映了基础性的缺陷：研究尚未建立将解释整合到端到端、人在环路系统中的方法论。

Method: 本文是一篇立场论文，通过分析近期ICML、NeurIPS和ICLR论文以及对XAI从业者的调查，揭示了限制累积性进展的重复性问题。

Result: 揭示了当前XAI领域存在的重复性问题：问题表述不清晰、评估目标不明确、缺乏解释驱动反馈的管线。

Conclusion: 机器学习社区必须从临时性XAI方法转向解决基础性和结构性挑战，并提出了一个实用清单，旨在将XAI转向更以人类为中心、行动导向的范式，为将解释整合到可操作、反馈驱动的AI系统中提供路线图。

Abstract: Despite the proliferation of Explainable AI (XAI) techniques -- from feature attributions to sparse autoencoders -- explanations rarely influence real-world workflows. In practice, they are often generated and discarded without guiding meaningful action. This gap reflects foundational shortcomings: research has not yet established methodologies for integrating explanations into end-to-end, human-in-the-loop systems. This position paper argues that the machine learning community must pivot from ad-hoc XAI methods toward addressing foundational & structural challenges, including unclear problem formulations, underspecified evaluation objectives, and the absence of pipelines for explanation-driven feedback. We support this claim through an analysis of recent ICML, NeurIPS, and ICLR papers and a survey of XAI practitioners, revealing recurring issues that limit cumulative progress. We conclude by outlining a practical checklist designed to shift XAI toward a more human-centered, action-oriented paradigm. By emphasizing foundational clarity over the development of ad-hoc methods, we hope to provide a roadmap for integrating explanations into actionable, feedback-driven AI systems.

</details>


### [155] [CARPRT: Class-Aware Zero-Shot Prompt Reweighting for Black-Box Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14125)
*Ruijiang Dong,Zesheng Ye,Jianzhong Qi,Lei Feng,Feng Liu,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CARPRT，一种无需训练的、类别感知的零样本提示重新加权方法，通过为每个类别单独计算提示权重来解决传统方法中提示权重与类别无关的问题，在多个图像分类基准上取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本图像分类方法在集成多个提示时，对所有类别使用相同的权重向量，忽略了不同提示对不同类别的重要性差异（如"空中视角"适合"机场"但不适合"苹果"），导致次优性能。

Method: CARPRT为每个类别标签独立计算提示权重：对于每个类别和每个提示，先利用该提示下被预测为该类别的图像，计算图像-文本相关性得分的平均值作为该类别的提示相关性度量，再对这些估计值进行归一化得到类别特定的权重向量。整个过程无需额外训练。

Result: 在标准图像分类基准上的实验表明，CARPRT优于现有的类别无关的重新加权方法，验证了建模提示-类别依赖关系对零样本预测的重要性。

Conclusion: 建模提示与类别之间的依赖关系对于有效的零样本预测至关重要，CARPRT通过类别感知的权重调整提升了VLM在零样本分类及更广泛的基于提示集成的应用中的性能。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) enable zero-shot image classification by computing the similarity score between an image and textual descriptions, typically formed by inserting a class label (e.g., "cat") into a prompt (e.g., "a photo of a"). Since the score for a given image-class pair is sensitive to the choice of prompt, existing studies ensemble multiple prompts using a weighting vector to aggregate scores across different prompts. Yet, in current strategies, the weighting vector assigned to each prompt is shared across all classes, implicitly assuming that prompts are conditionally independent of classes, which often does not hold in practice, as a prompt like "an aerial view of" might be apt for "airport" but ill-suited for "apple". To address this, we propose class-aware zero-shot prompt reweighting (CARPRT). This scoring scheme adjusts the weighting vector for each class label by capturing the class-specific relevance of different prompts in a training-free manner. For each class label and every available prompt, we quantify their class-specific relevance by averaging image-text relevance scores over images predicted to that class under the given prompt. These estimates are then normalized to derive class-specific weights. Evaluations on standard image classification benchmarks show that CARPRT outperforms existing class-independent reweighting methods, confirming that modeling prompt-class dependencies is crucial for effective zero-shot prediction and even broader VLM-based application settings that rely on prompt ensembling. Our code is available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/CARPRT.

</details>


### [156] [Explainable Geospatial AI for Satellite Ground Station Siting Using LiDAR-Derived Terrain Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14127)
*Shohini Sarkar,Smithi Mahendran,Rishi Chudasama,Varun Mannam,Arav Luthra,Yuvraj Rekhi,Vivek Nadig,Arsh Goenka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于机器学习的框架，利用开放地理空间数据预测代表性杂波高度（RCH），在美国地质调查局3DEP的LiDAR标签上训练，最终模型LightGBM的MAE=1.79m，R²=0.765，相比ITU-R P.452-18基线绝对误差降低超过60%，并通过SHAP分析揭示了树木冠层覆盖、土地覆盖语义和光谱反射率等关键预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代表性杂波高度（RCH）是无线电传播和干扰分析的关键参数，但当前ITU-R P.452-18推荐做法依赖固定杂波高度，忽略了同类土地覆盖内的变化，导致保守的排除区和低地球轨道地面站选址/频谱协调中的低效站点排名。

Method: 基于开放地理空间数据构建可解释、全球可部署的机器学习框架。使用USGS 3DEP LiDAR导出的标签，定义RCH为稳健的第75百分位杂波高度。整合全球土地覆盖、地形、人口、热红外和光学遥感产品作为推理特征。评估多种回归模型，最终选择LightGBM，并利用SHAP进行特征归因分析。

Result: 最终模型平均绝对误差（MAE）为1.79米，R²=0.765，绝对误差相比ITU基线降低超过60%。在米级误差、容忍带精度、过估/低估尾部、与ITU杂波高度体制的一致性以及基于SHAP的物理合理性等RF规划相关基准上表现良好。SHAP识别出树木冠层覆盖、土地覆盖语义和光谱反射率是最具影响力的预测因子。

Conclusion: 开放地理空间数据可以在不牺牲可解释性和可部署性的前提下，大规模改进杂波建模，优于传统固定杂波高度方法。

Abstract: Representative clutter height (RCH) is a key parameter in radio propagation and interference analysis because it captures the dominant height of local obstructions that drive terminal clutter loss. Current practice often relies on fixed clutter heights assigned to land use classes in Recommendation ITU-R P.452-18, but this misses within class variation and can lead to conservative exclusion zones and poor site ranking for low Earth orbit ground station siting and spectrum coordination. We present an interpretable, globally deployable machine learning framework for predicting RCH from open geospatial data. The model is trained using LiDAR derived labels from the U.S. Geological Survey 3D Elevation Program and inference time features from global land-cover, terrain, demographic, thermal, and optical remote sensing products. We define RCH using a robust 75th percentile clutter height statistic, evaluate multiple regressors, and select LightGBM for its accuracy, efficiency, and compatibility with feature attribution analysis. The final model achieves a mean absolute error of 1.79m and an R^2=0.765, reducing absolute error by more than 60% relative to the ITU baseline. Beyond aggregate fit, we evaluate domain facing criteria relevant to RF planning, including meter scale error, tolerance band accuracy, over and under estimation tails, agreement with ITU clutter height regimes, and SHAP-based physical plausibility. SHAP identifies tree canopy cover, land-cover semantics, and spectral reflectance as the most influential predictors. Studies on segmentation derived features, non-forest ablations, and land-cover matched international validation show that open geospatial data can improve clutter modeling at scale without sacrificing interpretability or deployability.

</details>


### [157] [Certified Domain Consistency for Multi-Domain Retrieval: Label-Free Per-Domain Contamination Control with Conformal Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14157)
*Jayakumar Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: C3R是一种即插即用的控制层，通过领域后验推断和两重分裂方案，在有限样本下保证每个领域的污染预算可证书化，否则弃权而非沉默违规；在最难领域保证污染降低而非紧界，实验表明边际控制在每个重采样中均违反最污染领域，而C3R从未违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨领域混合语料库检索时，相关但错误领域的证据会被排序指标遗漏，且共形风险控制边界只能勉强覆盖最差领域，导致严重欠覆盖。

Method: 提出C3R控制层：基于领域后验推断（无需查询时标签），对每个领域认证污染预算（可行时）否则弃权；核心是两重分裂方案，建立在风险控制预测集上，有限样本转移界从推断领域跨越到真实领域且斜率完全可估计，支持异质预算并反向推导用于部署。

Result: 在千次重采样校准中，证书从未违反（稳定性结果），而边际控制在每次抽取中都违反最污染领域；软降级在相等证书化污染下比最强校准级联保留更多召回率；结果在包括联邦法规独立测试集上可复现；LLM评判的下游探针显示错误权威接地随污染上升，在控制下下降。

Conclusion: C3R作为冻结栈、重排序器无关的控制层，能有效认证跨领域检索中的污染预算，在最具挑战的领域保证污染降低，优于传统的边际风险控制方法。

Abstract: Retrieval over corpora that mix several domains often returns relevant but wrong-domain evidence that ranking metrics miss and that conformal risk control bounds only marginally, under-covering the worst domains. This work introduces C3R, a drop-in control layer that, from an inferred domain posterior and no query-time label, certifies a per-domain contamination budget where feasible and otherwise abstains rather than silently violating; on the hardest domains it guarantees a reduction, not a tight bound. The core is a two-split scheme built on risk-controlling prediction sets, whose finite-sample transfer bound crosses from the inferred to the true domain with fully estimable slack, supports heterogeneous budgets, and inverts for deployment. Population validity rests on this bound and a controlled simulation; across a thousand resampled calibrations the certificate never violates (a stability result) while marginal control violates the most-contaminated domain in every draw, and soft demotion retains more recall than the strongest calibrated cascade at equal certified contamination. The method replicates across open testbeds including an independent one from public federal regulations, and an LLM-judged downstream probe indicates wrong-authority grounding rises with contamination and falls under control. The layer is frozen-stack and reranker-agnostic.

</details>


### [158] [QFireNet: A Quantum-Enhanced U-Net for Wildfire Segmentation from Sentinel-2 Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14160)
*Jaiman Munshi,Tanvi Tewary,Sawyer Bloom,Aidan Chu,Chetan Maviti,Kyon Winston-Bey,Harshit Badjatia,Farhan Kittur,Vardhan Madhavarapu,Varun Kota,Joshua Kwon,Nazia Rangwala-Vohra,Franz Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子-混合U-Net模型用于卫星遥感图像中的野火检测，通过在U-Net瓶颈层注入变分量子电路（QuFeX和QB-Net），并对比经典特征金字塔网络（FPN）。实验表明量子增强模型（QB-Net F1=31.18, QuFeX F1=30.79）优于经典U-Net基线（F1=28.71），而数据混合策略可消除域偏移，将FPN的F1提升至39.76。跨数据集验证（CaBuAr）证明了架构的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星图像野火检测面临类别不平衡、特征复杂性高、大气干扰等挑战，传统模型难以有效建模高维光谱特征空间。作者希望探索量子机器学习能否在语义分割任务中提供优势。

Method: 基于U-Net架构，在瓶颈层引入变分量子电路（QuFeX和QB-Net两种ansatz），形成量子-混合模型。同时构建经典FPN作为对比。还探索了经典改进：参数压缩、替代损失函数、输入数据均匀混合（消除地理域偏移）。跨数据集在CaBuAr上验证泛化性。

Result: 在匹配条件下，QB-Net（F1=31.18）和QuFeX（F1=30.79）优于经典U-Net基线（F1=28.71）。经典FPN得分为31.13。数据混合消除了训练/测试集间的域偏移，将FPN的F1提升至39.76。跨数据集验证表明架构具有良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 量子机器学习在野火图像分割任务中展现出潜在优势，当前结果初步验证了量子增强U-Net的有效性，未来需更多实验进一步验证和扩展这一发现。

Abstract: Wildfire detection from satellite imagery is a semantic image segmentation problem that has proven to be difficult due to challenges such as class imbalance, feature complexity, and atmospheric interference. In this paper, we build on the foundational U-Net image segmentation model to develop a quantum-hybrid solution in hopes of more effectively modeling the high-dimensional spectral feature space of the Sen2Fire dataset. We inject a variational quantum circuit in the bottleneck portion of U-Net, specifically the QuFeX and QB-Net ansatzes. We test a classical Feature Pyramid Network (FPN) for further comparative analysis of the model, and we also explore classical improvements to the U-Net model and its training process, including a compression of parameters, alternative loss functions, and uniform mixing of input data. Our primary finding is that under matched conditions, both QB-Net (with an $F_1$ score of 31.18) and QuFeX ($F_1 = 30.79$) outperformed the classical U-Net baseline results ($F_1 = 28.71$). Additionally, the classical FPN achieved a comparable score of 31.13. A crucial finding was that data mixing removed a significant domain shift between the geographically-separated train and test sets, which boosted the classical FPN $F_1$ score to 39.76. We validate the architecture's robustness and generalizability to the wildfire detection problem via cross-dataset transfer on the California Burned Areas (CaBuAr) dataset. Overall, we find that quantum machine learning has potential to provide an advantage in the problem of wildfire image segmentation, and further experiments will continue to validate and expand upon this finding.

</details>


### [159] [Branching Policy Optimization: Sandbox-Native Language Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14171)
*Bowei He,Yankai Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BPO 是一种针对可执行沙箱环境设计的强化学习算法，通过利用沙箱的确定性、可快照和可恢复特性，采用树状展开拓扑结构替代传统的独立轨迹采样，在共享前缀的分支间计算优势函数，从而在同等计算量下显著降低方差并提升 LLM 智能体在 WebShop、ALFWorld 和 SWE-bench 等任务上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLHF 类算法（如 PPO、RLOO、GRPO）在处理可执行沙箱环境时，仍然沿用“每个 prompt 独立采样 N 条轨迹”的拓扑结构，忽视了沙箱环境具有确定性、可快照和可恢复这一关键特性。这一特性允许从任意中间状态恢复执行，从而可以构建共享前缀的树状展开，实现方差共担，降低优势估计的方差。

Method: BPO 算法包含三个核心步骤：(i) 沿骨干轨迹在熵较高的决策点自适应地对沙箱进行快照；(ii) 在每个分支点分叉出 K 个替代动作并分别展开至终止状态；(iii) 利用兄弟分支的回报计算每步优势函数。论文证明了该估计器无偏，且方差严格低于传统轨迹级基线，方差缩减量等于前缀所能解释的回报方差部分。

Result: 在 WebShop、ALFWorld 和 SWE-bench Verified 上使用 Qwen2.5-7B 和 Llama-3.1-8B 作为骨干模型，BPO 相比 GRPO 和 RLOO 在同等计算量下取得了 3.6–6.1 个绝对百分点的成功率提升，梯度范数方差减半，并且仅需 38% 的策略更新次数即可达到最佳基线性能。

Conclusion: BPO 通过利用沙箱环境的确定性、可快照和可恢复特性，提出了一种全新的树状展开拓扑结构，能够更高效地利用计算资源进行策略优化，在多个具身推理任务上显著优于现有 RL 算法，为 LLM 智能体在交互式环境中的训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as the dominant paradigm for training large language model (LLM) agents that interact with executable sandboxes. State-of-the-art algorithms such as PPO, RLOO, and GRPO inherit their rollout topology from RLHF: for each prompt, N independent trajectories are sampled from the initial state, and an advantage is computed by subtracting a group baseline. This design ignores a defining property of agent sandboxes. They are deterministic, snapshottable, and resumable from any intermediate state. We argue that this property enables a fundamentally different rollout topology: rather than N independent trees of depth T, one can construct a single tree of N leaves whose siblings share prefixes, and therefore share variance. We instantiate this idea as Branching Policy Optimization (BPO), a sandbox-native RL algorithm that (i) adaptively snapshots the sandbox at high-entropy decision points along a backbone trajectory, (ii) forks K alternative actions per branch point and rolls out each to termination, and (iii) computes per-step advantages from sibling returns rather than from independent prompts. We prove this estimator is unbiased and has strictly lower variance than the trajectory-level baseline, with the reduction equal to the prefix-explained portion of return variance. On WebShop, ALFWorld, and SWE-bench Verified with Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B backbones, BPO improves success by 3.6--6.1 absolute points over GRPO and RLOO at matched compute, halves gradient-norm variance, and matches the best baseline using 38% fewer policy updates.

</details>


### [160] [How Much of a 10-K Matters? Aggregation-Dependent Value of Full-Text versus Risk-Factor Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14174)
*Sanggyu Sean Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向10-K年度报告（含Item 1A风险因素章节）的有监督情感词典学习方法，利用收益和波动率标签在三个聚合层级（行业、投资组合、个股）训练情感分数。在94家纳斯达克100科技公司1383份文件（2006-2023）上的实验表明：全文情感在行业和投资组合层面更准确，而Item 1A在个股层面表现更好；Loughran-McDonald词典基准与价格强负相关，凸显了有监督方法在监管披露文本中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融情感提取主要依赖新闻文本和仅针对收益标签的有监督方法，对10-K年度报告及其最适于预测波动率的风险披露文本探索不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究监管披露文本的情感提取方法及聚合层级影响。

Method: 将有监督词典学习方法扩展到10-K全文和Item 1A风险因素章节，同时使用收益和波动率标签训练情感分数。在三个聚合层级（行业、投资组合、个股）上评估，利用1383份文件（94家纳斯达克100科技公司，2006-2023）进行分类准确率、与市场结果的相关性及词汇内容分析。

Result: 全文情感在行业和投资组合层面更准确，但个股层面Item 1A表现更好（归因于文档规模与训练信号量的交互）。Loughran-McDonald词典基准在所有层级与价格强负相关，表明有监督方法的必要性。

Conclusion: 这些发现为后续更大规模多源情感生成系统奠定了方法论基础，明确了监管披露文本情感提取的设计选择（如聚合层级与文本范围的选择需匹配训练信号强度）。

Abstract: Financial sentiment extraction has largely relied on news text and supervised extraction against return labels alone, leaving 10-K filings -- and volatility, the target risk disclosure is arguably best suited to informing -- comparatively unexplored. We extend a supervised lexicon-learning approach to 10-K filings and their Item 1A risk-factor sections, training sentiment scores against both return and volatility labels at three levels of aggregation: sector, portfolio, and individual firm. Across 1,383 filings from 94 Nasdaq-100 technology constituents (2006--2023), we evaluate the resulting twelve sentiment metrics on classification accuracy, correlation with realised market outcomes, and qualitative lexical content. Full-filing text produces more accurate sentiment at the sector and portfolio level for both targets, but this reverses at the individual-firm level, where the narrower Item 1A section performs better -- an effect we attribute to the interaction between document volume and the amount of independent training signal available at each level of aggregation. A Loughran-McDonald dictionary baseline is consistently, strongly negatively correlated with price at every level tested, underscoring the value of a supervised approach for regulatory disclosure text. These findings, and the design choices they motivate, establish the sentiment-generation methodology underlying a subsequent, larger-scale, multi-source system.

</details>


### [161] [Low-Latency Relay Selection in NR-V2X Vehicular Communications via Graph Isomorphism Networks with Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14176)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Emiliano Pallotti,Simone Angelini,Pierpaolo Salvo,Paola Vocca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种边缘感知的学习优化框架（GINE）用于C-V2X中继选择，通过图神经网络模仿MILP决策，实现毫秒级推理，并混合GP-MILP策略在保持最优解的同时大幅降低求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C-V2X在密集城市环境中需要可靠低延迟上行链路，但直连链路常因信道变化和阻塞而退化。多跳中继可恢复覆盖，但中继链路激活在无线、容量和路由约束下是NP-hard问题，传统MILP求解器运行时间随图大小增长而急剧增加，无法满足NR-V2X的严格延迟预算。

Method: 将每个V2X快照建模为有向图，节点特征编码车辆状态和流量需求，边特征编码无线链路容量。使用离线MILP最优解监督训练图同构网络（GINE）进行边级中继激活预测，单次前向传播即可获得结果。进一步提出混合GP-MILP策略，用GINE预测剪枝MILP搜索空间，加速求解。

Result: 在OSM-SUMO-GEMV^2管道生成的大规模数据集上，GINE在链路级别匹配MILP决策（准确率0.9589，F1-score 0.9544），相比1跳MILP基线实现端到端连通性增益（4个RSU时达9.2%，2个RSU时达12%）。推理延迟严格控制在5ms以内。GP-MILP保持与MILP等价的目标值，同时超过98%的实例求解时间低于30ms。

Conclusion: 所提框架使MILP级别的优化能够在NR-V2X严格的延迟预算内实现，为实时中继选择提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reliable, low-latency uplink connectivity is a key requirement for C-V2X networks in dense urban environments, where fast channel variations and blockages often degrade direct vehicle-to-infrastructure links. Multi-hop relaying can restore coverage, but relay-link activation under radio, capacity, and routing constraints results in an NP-hard optimisation problem, typically solved via Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), whose runtime scales poorly with graph size. This paper introduces an edge-aware Learning-to-Optimise framework for real-time relay selection. Each V2X snapshot is modelled as a directed graph: node features encode vehicle state and traffic demand, while edge features capture radio-link capacity. An offline MILP oracle generates optimal relay configurations that supervise a Graph Isomorphism Network with Edge Features (GINE), enabling edge-level relay activation through a single forward pass, with tightly bounded inference latency. To bridge learning and exact optimisation, we also propose a hybrid GINE-Pruned MILP (GP-MILP) strategy in which GINE predictions prune the MILP search space. Experiments on a large-scale dataset generated via an OSM-SUMO-GEMV$^2$ pipeline show that GINE closely matches MILP decisions at the link level (accuracy 0.9589), F1-score (0.9544) on validation) and yields consistent end-to-end connectivity gains over a 1-hop MILP baseline (up to 9.2% with four RSUs and 12% with two RSUs). Inference latency remains tightly bounded, with all evaluated instances completing within 5~ms. Moreover, GP-MILP preserves MILP-equivalent solutions (same objective value) while achieving solver runtimes below 30~ms for more than 98%) of the graph instances, making MILP-grade optimisation compatible with stringent NR-V2X latency budgets.

</details>


### [162] [RENEW: Towards Learning World Models and Repairing Model Exploitation from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14180)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Mykel Kochenderfer,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出从人类反馈中学习动力学（DLHF），通过人类对想象轨迹的偏好来直接修复离线强化学习中世界模型的利用问题，并引入RENEW方法提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中的世界模型在数据覆盖稀疏时容易遭受模型利用。先前的工作要么通过收集更多专家演示（昂贵、不安全或不可用）来解决，要么采用保守算法避免不确定区域（限制泛化能力）。因此需要一种既能修复利用问题又不依赖额外专家数据的新方法。

Method: 形式化为从人类反馈中学习动力学（DLHF），使用Bradley-Terry偏好损失对轨迹对数似然进行优化。针对朴素DLHF样本效率低的问题，提出RENEW，利用认知不确定性聚焦于最易被利用的区域进行微调。

Result: 在Jumanji和经典控制环境上的实验表明，朴素DLHF需要大量偏好预算，而RENEW显著提升了样本效率，限制了灾难性遗忘，并减少了预训练世界模型中的利用问题。

Conclusion: 研究表明人类偏好可以直接监督世界模型动力学，为离线模型基强化学习中的利用问题提供了一种新的解决途径。

Abstract: World models are widely used in offline reinforcement learning (RL) to improve sample efficiency and generate experience beyond a fixed dataset. However, they are vulnerable to model exploitation where data coverage is thin. Prior work addresses this either by collecting more expert demonstrations, which is often expensive, unsafe, or unavailable, or by conservative algorithms that avoid uncertain regions, which limits generalization. We propose instead to repair exploitation directly using human preferences over imagined rollouts, leveraging the strong intuitive physics that allows humans to easily spot egregious dynamics hallucinations. We formalize this as Dynamics Learning from Human Feedback (DLHF), a Bradley-Terry preference loss over trajectory log-likelihoods under a learned dynamics model. Unfortunately, naive DLHF is sample inefficient, so we introduce RENEW, which uses epistemic uncertainty to focus finetuning where the model is most exploitable. We evaluate on several Jumanji and classic control environments and find that while naive DLHF requires an outsize preference budget, RENEW makes the framework practical by improving sample efficiency, limiting catastrophic forgetting, and reducing exploitation in pretrained world models. Taken together, our results provide initial evidence that preferences can supervise world model dynamics directly, offering a new approach to addressing exploitation in offline model-based RL.

</details>


### [163] [Closed-Loop Knowledge Dynamics: An Operational Framework for Saturation and Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14185)
*Xuening Wu,Shan Yu,Shenqin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了闭环知识系统在反复内部反馈下性能饱和的原因，并提出一个三层操作框架，通过结构干预（改变转移核参数θ）来逃离吸引子。利用Lyapunov漂移条件证明了稳定内部动力学趋近有界稳定区域，并给出了逃离吸引子的度量条件（吸引子位移和KL下界）。案例研究（LLM代码修复、稀疏奖励强化学习、贝叶斯优化）展示了反馈强度和一致性如何影响质量提升式逃离。贡献在于连接了稳定性工具、可测量的干预效应和跨领域诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为什么闭环知识系统在反复内部反馈下会饱和？需要理解其机制，并找出何种外部信息能帮助系统逃离当前吸引子，实现持续改进。

Method: 提出三层操作框架：知识状态通过转移核演化，结构参数θ的观测等价类定义治理结构，吸引子和盆地是固定θ动力学的性质。结构干预通过改变θ产生可检测的核差异，使结构变化可证伪。使用Lyapunov漂移条件分析稳定性，通过吸引子位移的度量条件和基线相对KL下界刻画逃离。

Result: 稳定内部动力学以指数衰减瞬态和噪声控制残差趋近有界稳定区域；结构干预可导致吸引子位移，且KL下界可提高逃离概率；条件互信息不能保证逃离，因为它衡量干预条件更新之间的变异而非偏离无干预定律。案例研究证实了反馈强度和一致性对质量提升式逃离的影响。

Conclusion: 本文建立了稳定性工具、可测量干预效应和跨领域诊断之间的操作连接，为理解和改进闭环学习系统提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Feedback-driven loops support iterative improvement in large language models, reinforcement learning, and autonomous discovery, yet their gains often diminish under repeated internal feedback. We study why closed-loop knowledge systems saturate and what external information can move them beyond their current attractors. We introduce a three-level operational framework in which knowledge states $x_t$ evolve through transition kernels $K_θ$ indexed by a structural parameter $θ$. The governing structure is defined as the observational equivalence class of $θ$ induced by these kernels, while attractors and basins are properties of the fixed-$θ$ dynamics. A structural intervention changes $θ$ and produces a detectable kernel discrepancy on pre-specified probe states, making structural change falsifiable. Using a Lyapunov drift condition, we show that stable internal dynamics approach bounded stability regions with exponentially attenuated transients and a noise-controlled residual floor. We characterize escape through a metric condition on intervention-induced attractor displacement and a baseline-relative KL lower bound for increasing escape probability. This analysis also explains why conditional mutual information alone cannot certify escape: it measures variation among intervention-conditioned updates rather than departure from the no-intervention law. Case studies in LLM code repair, sparse-reward reinforcement learning, and Bayesian optimization use matched continuation controls to illustrate how feedback strength and alignment affect quality-improving escape. Our contribution is an operational connection among stability tools, measurable intervention effects, and cross-domain diagnostics.

</details>


### [164] [A Temporal Machine Learning-Based Time-to-Event Model for Predicting ALS Progression and Healthcare Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14190)
*Zongliang Yue,Qi Li,Terry Heiman-Patterson,Frank Bearoff,Zhaohui Qin,Huanmei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于数字孪生思想的时间-事件预测框架，用于肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）患者的功能衰退和辅助设备使用（如轮椅）的个性化预测。该框架整合纵向ALSFRS-R评分、日常生活活动能力和人口统计学数据，通过相关聚类识别功能域，使用广义加性混合模型描述非线性衰退，并采用时序机器学习模型进行动态预测。Cox比例风险模型表明下肢功能（尤其是行走和爬楼梯）是轮椅需求的最强预测因子。最终的数字孪生时间-事件模型能够生成个性化生存曲线并动态预测无轮椅生存期，为临床决策和试验分层提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALS是一种异质性神经退行性疾病，预测临床重要里程碑（如辅助设备使用）极具挑战性。现有方法难以有效地将功能衰退轨迹与个性化决策支持相结合，因此需要一个可扩展、可解释且临床可操作的框架来链接疾病进展与个体化管理。

Method: 1. 构建标准化的纵向数据集（整合诊断记录、ALSFRS-R评估、日常生活活动能力及人口统计学信息）；2. 基于相关聚类识别功能域（延髓、上肢、轴向、下肢、呼吸）；3. 使用广义加性混合模型（GAMM）刻画非线性功能衰退；4. 开发时序机器学习模型预测纵向功能下降及阶段依赖性进展；5. 应用Cox比例风险模型确定轮椅接入的关键预测因子；6. 构建数字孪生启发的时序机器学习时间-事件（TTE）模型，生成个性化生存曲线并动态预测无轮椅生存期。

Result: 下肢功能（特别是行走和爬楼梯）是更早需要轮椅的最强预测因子；数字孪生TTE模型能够生成个性化生存曲线并动态预测无轮椅生存期；该框架在ALS进展预测和个性化决策支持方面表现出可扩展性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生框架提供了一种可扩展、可解释且临床可操作的方法，将ALS进展与个性化决策支持相结合，适用于主动护理规划、临床试验分层和精准医学。

Abstract: Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS) is a progressive and heterogeneous neurodegenerative disease in which predicting clinically meaningful milestones, such as assistive device use, remains challenging. We developed a time-to-event, digital-twin-inspired framework that integrates longitudinal ALS Functional Rating Scale-Revised (ALSFRS-R) trajectories with survival modeling to support individualized prediction of functional decline and assistive device utilization. We constructed a harmonized longitudinal dataset by integrating diagnosis records, ALSFRS-R assessments, activities of daily living, and demographic information, followed by preprocessing to ensure data quality, temporal alignment, and cohort consistency. Correlation-based clustering identified coherent functional domains spanning bulbar, upper limb, axial, lower limb, and respiratory systems. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear, domain-specific functional decline across all domains. In addition, a temporal machine learning model was developed to predict longitudinal functional decline and capture stage-dependent disease progression. Cox proportional hazards modeling further identified lower limb function, particularly walking and stair climbing, as the strongest predictors of earlier wheelchair access. Building on these results, we implemented a digital twin-inspired temporal machine learning-based time-to-event (TTE) model that generates individualized survival curves and dynamically predicts wheelchair-free survival. This framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and clinically actionable approach for linking ALS progression with personalized decision support, with applications in proactive care planning, clinical trial stratification, and precision medicine.

</details>


### [165] [TEDDY: A Pediatric Foundation Model for Risk Forewarning from ICD-Coded Diagnostic Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14191)
*Matthew Brady Neeley,Jorge Botas,Johnathan Jia,Lin Yao,Daniel Palacios,Benjamin Choi,Zhandong Liu,Hyun-Hwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TEDDY是一个仅184万参数的解码器Transformer，在单中心约7300万条儿童ICD-10诊断记录上训练，能预测797种疾病的首次发作时间（中位AUC 72.0%），优于多种基线模型，且在低患病率疾病、长时程预测（超过2年）上表现稳健，展示了无需大规模数据和千亿参数即可实现儿科诊断轨迹生成式建模的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科电子健康记录包含结构化的发育临床轨迹，但其用于生成式医疗基础模型的潜力尚未被充分探索；现有模型通常需要大规模数据和海量参数，本研究旨在验证仅用单中心儿科数据和小型模型即可实现广泛、罕见病及长时程风险预测。

Method: 使用1.84M参数的解码器Transformer（TEDDY），在来自1.6百万儿童、约7300万条ICD-10诊断记录上训练；模型建模纵向诊断序列与就诊时间；预测时遮盖就诊代码（仅首次出现）并与性别-年龄匹配对照比较；在797个疾病发病预测任务（覆盖16个ICD-10章节）上评估；与DenseNet、CNN、RNN、LSTM及通用大语言模型对比。

Result: 中位AUC 72.0%，在96-99%的任务上优于基线模型（DenseNet 50.0%, CNN 57.2%, RNN 60.1%, LSTM 62.7%）；性能在不同性别和年龄组一致，在低患病率诊断中最强（最罕见的225种疾病中202种（90%）95%置信区间高于随机）；预测信号在首次诊断前超过2年可检测（中位AUC 59.7% unrestricted，64.4% fixed-cohort）；哮喘AUC 79.3%，ADHD AUC 84.7%（对比最强基线62.7%和71.7%）；就诊时间预测365天平均绝对受限生存时间误差3.0天。

Conclusion: 儿科诊断历史可作为紧凑型生成式模型的基材，支持广泛、罕见病和长时程风险预测，无需人群级数据或千亿参数模型；证明了小型模型在儿科医疗预测中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Pediatric electronic health records capture developmentally structured clinical trajectories, yet their potential for generative healthcare foundation models remains largely unexplored. Here we present TEDDY (Temporal Event Decoder for Disease in Youth), a 1.84-million-parameter decoder transformer trained on approximately 73 million ICD-10 diagnoses from 1.6 million children at a single pediatric institution. TEDDY models longitudinal diagnosis trajectories and visit timing. Predictions were made before visit codes were revealed, limited to first occurrences, and evaluated against sex- and age-matched controls. Across 797 disease-onset prediction tasks spanning 16 ICD-10 chapters, TEDDY achieved a median AUC of 72.0%, outperforming same-data DenseNet (50.0%), CNN (57.2%), RNN (60.1%), and LSTM (62.7%) baselines on 96-99% of tasks. Performance held across sex and age and was strongest among lower-prevalence diagnoses; 202 of the 225 rarest conditions (90%) had 95% confidence intervals above chance. Predictive signal remained detectable more than two years before first recorded diagnosis, with median AUCs of 59.7% in the unrestricted analysis and 64.4% in a fixed-cohort sensitivity analysis. In asthma and attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder benchmarks, AUCs were 79.3% and 84.7%, compared with 62.7% and 71.7% for the strongest comparators, including a general-purpose language model three orders of magnitude larger. Visit-timing predictions had a 3.0-day mean absolute restricted mean survival-time error over 365 days, although median and long-tail return intervals remained miscalibrated. Together, these results establish pediatric diagnostic histories as a substrate for compact generative models supporting broad, rare-disease, and long-horizon risk forecasting without population-scale data or billion-parameter models.

</details>


### [166] [Long-term User Engagement Optimization through Model-agnostic Downstream Rewards Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14192)
*Dingsu Wang,Filip Ryzner,Kelly He,Armando Ordorica,David Woo,Aditya Mantha,Liyao Lu,Usha Amrutha Nookala,Haoran Guo,Jiacong He,Olafur Gudmundsson,Matt Chun,Krystal Benitez,Dhruvil Deven Badani,Yijie Dylan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一、模型无关的下游奖励框架，用于在大规模推荐系统中优化长期用户价值。通过离线筛选框架识别可早期观测且预测留存率的会话级行为，推导出多种模型无关的下游奖励信号，并在Pinterest多个场景（首页、相关Pin、搜索、通知）部署，在线A/B实验显示参与度和留存指标一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统的优化目标从短期行为信号转向长期用户参与和留存，但直接优化留存面临回报信号稀疏、延迟且仅部分可归因于早期推荐的挑战。现有工作（序列建模、强化学习）需要特定任务的奖励工程、大量计算开销且难以泛化，亟需一个统一、通用的框架。

Method: (1) 形式化下游奖励学习问题；(2) 构建离线筛选框架，识别早期可观测且预测未来留存的会话级行为；(3) 从多源用户行为模式中推导出多种模型无关的下游奖励信号；(4) 讨论工程化部署的挑战与经验。

Result: 在线A/B实验在参与度和留存相关指标上取得一致改进，该框架已成功部署至Pinterest的Homefeed、Related Pins、Search、Notifications等多个表面。

Conclusion: 本文提出的模型无关下游奖励框架有效解决了长期用户价值优化中奖励稀疏和延迟的问题，具备通用性和可扩展性，为大规模推荐系统的长期目标优化提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: As recommender systems mature in the past few years, their optimization objectives have evolved from a primary focusing on short-term behavioral signals to a broader emphasis on long-term user engagement and retention. However, directly optimizing retention is difficult because return signals are sparse, delayed, and only partially attributable to earlier recommendations. Prior work has addressed this challenge with sequential modeling and reinforcement learning, but these approaches typically require task specific reward engineering, substantial computational overhead, and surface specific implementations that are difficult to generalize.
